Result: 在 FLUX.1-dev、Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 和 CogVideoX-2B 上实验表明，效率-保真度权衡优于所评估的训练无关缓存基线；兼容 TeaCache、DiCache、MagCache 等多种基础代理，在延迟几乎不变的情况下持续提升保真度。

Conclusion: RA-CFGCache 通过显式建模 CFG 下误差组合与时间步依赖的误差传播影响，有效解决了现有缓存方法的两个失配问题，是一种通用、模型无关且代理无关的缓存改进框架，可稳定提升训练无关缓存方法的生成质量与效率权衡。

Abstract: Diffusion models enable high-quality visual generation, but iterative denoising remains computationally expensive, especially under classifier-free guidance (CFG), which requires both conditional and unconditional evaluations. Training-free caching reduces this cost by reuse of previously computed features or predictions. However, existing branch-local reuse criteria do not explicitly account for how cache errors combine under CFG or how local perturbations affect the final output. We identify two misalignments in cache control: a branch-guided mismatch, where guided error depends on both the magnitudes and alignment of branch errors, and a local-final mismatch, where the downstream impact of a local error varies across timesteps. We propose RA-CFGCache, a Risk-Aligned Caching framework under CFG that incorporates both factors while keeping the sampling schedule and guidance rule fixed. CFG-aware Guided-Risk Composition combines existing branch-wise proxies using CFG coefficients and offline-calibrated cross-branch alignment. Propagation-Aware Rescaling further weights the resulting guided-risk estimate with a timestep-dependent propagation prior calibrated from isolated reuse perturbations. An online threshold controller then determines when to jointly refresh or reuse both branches. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev, Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, and CogVideoX-2B demonstrate improved efficiency--fidelity trade-offs over evaluated training-free caching baselines. Moreover, RA-CFGCache is compatible with diverse base proxy families, including TeaCache-, DiCache-, and MagCache-style estimators, and consistently improves fidelity at nearly unchanged latency. Code is available at https://github.com/yiming-l21/RA-CFGCache.git.

</details>


### [34] [Merlin Plus: A Large-Scale, Multi-Cancer, Image-Mask-Report Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36436)
*Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Wenxuan Li,Szymon Plotka,Ruby Honjol,Jakub Przado,Xinze Zhou,Kang Wang,Yang Yang,Malte Jensen,Akshay S. Chaudhari,Curtis P. Langlotz,Alan L. Yuille,Zongwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Merlin Plus 是首个包含 9 个器官、由放射科医生标注的肿瘤掩码的大规模 CT 数据集，在 Merlin 基础上新增 1,153 个体素级肿瘤掩码和纵向元数据，以解决多癌分割中肿瘤掩码稀缺的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多癌症 CT 分割受到不同器官肿瘤掩码稀缺的根本限制。现有数据集缺乏大规模、跨器官、由放射科医生标注的肿瘤掩码，同时缺乏支持癌症进展时序建模的纵向元数据，制约了可扩展的多器官癌症检测与分割研究。

Method: 提出基于报告（report-based）的主动学习框架：利用放射学报告识别适合标注的肿瘤病例，并辅助训练肿瘤分割模型；模型生成初始掩码，放射科医生进行审查与修正得到最终掩码，从而在保证标注质量的同时降低标注负担。最终构建出涵盖 9 个器官、1,153 个体素级肿瘤掩码及纵向元数据的 Merlin Plus 数据集。

Result: 构建了首个大规模多器官 CT 肿瘤分割数据集 Merlin Plus，包含 9 个器官、1,153 个放射科医生审核的体素级肿瘤掩码，并带有纵向元数据；数据集已公开于 https://github.com/MrGiovanni/MerlinPlus。

Conclusion: Merlin Plus 直接解决了多癌分割掩码稀缺这一主要瓶颈，为 CT 中可扩展的多器官癌症检测、分割和纵向分析提供了支持。

Abstract: Multi-cancer segmentation in computed tomography (CT) is fundamentally limited by the scarcity of tumor masks across different organs. We present Merlin Plus, the first large-scale CT dataset with radiologist-created tumor masks across 9 organs. Merlin Plus extends the Merlin dataset by adding 1,153 per-voxel tumor masks and longitudinal metadata. To create these tumor masks, we developed a report-based active-learning framework in which radiology reports identify tumor cases for annotation and support training of a tumor segmentation model. The model generates initial masks, which radiologists review and correct to produce the final masks, reducing annotation burden while maintaining high-quality annotations. Besides tumor masks, the longitudinal metadata in Merlin Plus enables temporal modeling of cancer progression. By directly addressing the major bottleneck of limited multi-cancer segmentation masks, Merlin Plus supports scalable multi-organ cancer detection, segmentation, and longitudinal analysis in CT. Dataset is available at: https://github.com/MrGiovanni/MerlinPlus

</details>


### [35] [DARE to Mitigate Hallucination: Dual-path Auto-Regressive-aware Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36440)
*Jae-Ho Lee,Jeong-Eun Lee,Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对大视觉语言模型（LVLM）中的物体幻觉问题，本文发现仅靠基于教师强制（TF）的表示编辑不足以反映自回归（AR）解码动态，因此提出 DARE（双路径自回归感知编辑）框架，融合文本对比、图像对比与 AR 感知表示信号，在多个基准上一致降低幻觉并保持感知能力与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM 虽在多模态任务中取得显著进展，但物体幻觉（生成与视觉输入不一致的描述）依然顽固存在。现有免训练表示编辑方法通常基于教师强制（TF）下幻觉与真实响应的对比构造方向，但 LVLM 实际生成采用自回归（AR）解码，质疑 TF 分析是否充分反映导致幻觉的生成动态，因此需要探究并弥合 TF 编辑与实际 AR 生成行为之间的差距。

Method: 提出 DARE（Dual-path Auto-Regressive-aware Editing）混合幻觉编辑框架，整合两条互补的对比路径与自回归感知表示信号。具体从三方面构造幻觉编辑方向：(1) 基于 TF 的文本对比；(2) 解码过程中的 AR 感知表示转移；(3) 成对图像之间受控的视觉差异（图像对比）。通过将文本对比、图像对比及 AR 感知信号混合，实现对幻觉相关表示的编辑。

Result: 在多个 LVLM 幻觉基准上的大量实验表明，DARE 能够一致地减少物体幻觉，同时保持多模态感知能力与推理效率，验证了融合 AR 感知与双路径对比的有效性。

Conclusion: TF 编辑单独不足以捕捉与幻觉相关的解码动态与多模态交互，而 DARE 通过整合 TF 文本对比、AR 感知转移与图像对比，提供了更完整的幻觉编辑方案，有效缓解物体幻觉且不牺牲感知能力与效率。代码已开源。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently achieved remarkable progress across multimodal tasks, yet object hallucination remains a persistent challenge where models generate descriptions inconsistent with the visual input. Recent work mitigates hallucinations through training-free representation editing, typically by constructing hallucination-related directions from teacher-forcing (TF) contrasts between hallucinated and truthful responses. However, LVLMs operate through autoregressive (AR) decoding during generation, raising the question of whether TF-based analysis fully reflects the generation dynamics that lead to hallucinated outputs. In this paper, we analyze the relationship between TF-based editing and AR generation behavior and find that TF-based editing alone may be insufficient to capture both decoding dynamics and multimodal interactions associated with hallucinations. To address this limitation, we propose DARE (Dual-path Auto-Regressive-aware Editing), a hybrid hallucination editing framework that integrates two complementary contrast pathways: textual contrasts and image contrasts, together with autoregressive-aware representation signals. Specifically, DARE constructs hallucination editing directions from (1) TF-based textual contrasts, (2) AR-aware representation transitions during decoding, and (3) controlled visual differences between paired images. Extensive experiments on multiple LVLM hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that DARE consistently reduces object hallucinations while preserving multimodal perception capability and inference efficiency. Our implementation code is available at https://github.com/KU-VGI/DARE.

</details>


### [36] [Online Versatile Incremental Learning: Towards Class and Domain-Agnostic Adaptation at Any Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36442)
*Jae-Ho Lee,Min-Yeong Park,Jun-Yeong Moon,Jung Uk Kim,Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Online VIL（在线多面增量学习）场景，并设计 TopFlow 框架，通过域无关流匹配（DFM）与全局拓扑保持（GTP）两种机制，在类与域同时、连续且无边界变化的动态环境中实现先进的持续学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法无法同时应对类别与视觉域的连续、并发且无显式边界的变化，而真实部署环境正需要这种能力。为此，作者提出更贴近现实的 Online VIL 场景，以填补这一空白。

Method: 提出 TopFlow 框架，包含两个互补机制：(1) 域无关流匹配（DFM），将测地流核（geodesic flow kernel）集成到对比学习中，引导模型学习域无关的表征；(2) 全局拓扑保持（GTP），在不显式存储历史样本的情况下维持特征空间的全局结构。

Result: 大量实验表明，TopFlow 有效解决了现有方法在 Online VIL 场景下的不足，并在具有挑战性的 Online VIL 任务中取得了最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: TopFlow 验证了在类与域同时动态演化的环境中保持表征质量与全局结构的重要性，为构建真实动态环境下的持续学习系统提供了可行方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: Continual learning enables vision systems to adapt to ever-changing data distributions. Despite significant advances, existing approaches fail to capture continuous and concurrent shifts in classes and domains, a critical capability for real-world deployment. This work introduces Online VIL (Online Versatile Incremental Learning), a novel scenario where class concepts and visual domains evolve simultaneously online without explicit boundaries. To better adapt to the challenges of such dynamic environments that more closely resemble real-world conditions, we propose a novel framework TopFlow, Topology preservation with Flow matching representation that contains two complementary mechanisms: Domain-agnostic Flow Matching (DFM) and Global Topology Preservation (GTP). DFM guides the model to have domain-agnostic representations by integrating the geodesic flow kernel into contrastive learning. In contrast, GTP maintains the global structure of the feature space without explicitly storing past examples. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that TopFlow effectively addresses the limitations of existing methods within the Online VIL scenario, achieving state-of-the-art performance in challenging Online VIL. The proposed methods suggest potential directions for building continual learning systems in realistic dynamic environments. Our implementation code is available at https://github.com/KU-VGI/Online-VIL.

</details>


### [37] [DynamicHOI: Coupled Dynamics for Physics-aware HOI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36454)
*Wenliang Guo,Zhanbo Huang,Yu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynamicHOI 是一个物理感知的手-物交互重建框架，结合几何引导的扩散精修与手-物耦合动力学，通过接触力传递和主动手部致动建模，抑制力学上不合理的手-物交互轨迹，并在三个数据集上验证了重建效果及下游应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目 RGB 视频的手-物交互（HOI）重建方法主要关注视觉与几何一致性，但在部分观测条件下可能产生视觉上合理、力学上却不一致的运动轨迹，缺乏对底层交互动力学的约束，导致重建结果在物理层面不可靠。

Method: 提出 DynamicHOI，将几何引导的扩散精修与手-物耦合动力学相结合。几何信息在空间上锚定视觉证据用于轨迹精修；同时利用关节逆动力学与牛顿-欧拉动力学分别推导手的广义力和物体旋量（wrench），施加动力学层面的监督。进一步通过接触力传递耦合手与物体的动力学，并恢复主动手部致动作为交互层面的物理量，将其经验幅值分布建模为概率先验，惩罚不可能的致动并抑制力学上不合理的重建运动。

Result: 在三个 HOI 数据集上的实验表明，DynamicHOI 在手的重建和物体重建两方面均取得一致提升。重建出的轨迹还能进一步服务于下游应用，包括手部世界模型生成与机器人操作学习。

Conclusion: 物理感知的 HOI 建模不仅能够提升手-物交互重建的力学合理性与精度，还能惠及下游任务，验证了其超越单纯重建的应用价值。

Abstract: We study hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction from monocular RGB videos, where partial observations can produce visually plausible yet mechanically inconsistent trajectories. Existing methods mainly enforce visual and geometric agreement, leaving the underlying interaction dynamics insufficiently constrained. We propose DynamicHOI, a physics-aware HOI reconstruction framework combining geometry-grounded diffusion refinement with coupled hand-object dynamics. Geometry spatially grounds visual evidence for trajectory refinement, while articulated inverse dynamics and Newton-Euler dynamics derive hand generalized forces and object wrenches for dynamics-level supervision. We further couple hand and object dynamics through contact-force transfer and recover active hand actuation as an interaction-level physical quantity. We formulate its empirical magnitude distribution into a probabilistic prior that penalizes unlikely actuation and suppresses mechanically implausible reconstructed motion. Experiments on three HOI datasets show consistent improvements in both hand and object reconstruction. The reconstructed trajectories further benefit downstream applications including hand world-model generation and robotic manipulation learning, demonstrating the value of physics-aware HOI modeling beyond reconstruction.

</details>

*Yicong Li,Junjie Wang,Leander Lauenburg,Ella Hugie,Alexandra Irger,Wanhua Li,Donglai Wei,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对视觉-语言模型（VLM）在连接组学电子显微镜图像标注决策任务上进行基准测试，涵盖突触检测（有无与极性）和校对（切分错误与合并错误）两类任务，系统比较了开放/闭源模型在零样本、少样本上下文学习和LoRA微调下的表现，并与专用模型对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组学中电子显微镜图像标注（突触检测与校对）高度依赖专家人力、成本高昂。作者希望探究通用视觉-语言模型能否承担或辅助这些标注决策，从而减轻人工负担并加速大规模神经回路重建。

Method: 作者构建了基于公开资源的突触检测数据集，对19个开放模型和2个闭源模型（覆盖多种架构与规模）在零样本、四样本上下文学习及LoRA微调三种设置下进行评测，并与专用（specialist）模型对比。校对任务则在ConnectomeBench2数据集上对3个开放和2个闭源模型评测，并进行从果蝇、小鼠到人类、斑马鱼的跨物种迁移测试。

Result: 多数模型零样本表现接近随机水平；少量示例主要帮助闭源模型和最大的开放模型提升。用数千个标签做LoRA微调后，开放模型达到与专用模型相当的水平。在未见物种上评测时，最佳适配的VLM在识别合并错误方面超过了用相同数据训练的专用模型。

Conclusion: 通用视觉-语言模型经过适度微调即可在连接组学标注决策上媲美甚至（在跨物种合并错误识别上）超越专用模型，展示了VLM作为连接组学辅助标注工具的潜力。项目将在录用后公开。

Abstract: We benchmarked vision-language models (VLMs) on the decisions annotators take when inspecting electron microscopy images in connectomics: synapse detection (presence and polarity) and proofreading (split errors and merge errors). For synapse detection, we evaluated 19 open and 2 closed models across various architectures and sizes under zero-shot, four-shot in-context learning and LoRA settings, against specialist models, on datasets constructed by us using public resources. For proofreading, we evaluated 3 open and 2 closed models on the ConnectomeBench2 dataset, with cross-species transfer from fly and mouse to human and zebrafish. Most models were at chance zero-shot; a few examples helped mainly the closed and largest open ones. LoRA on a few thousand labels brought open models level with specialist models. When evaluated on unseen species, the best adapted VLMs outperformed specialist models trained on the same data in identifying merge errors. The project will be publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [39] [Reimagine Video Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36496)
*Yu Yuan,Yawen Lu,Guoxian Song,Kevin Duarte,Ratheesh Kalarot,Di Chang,Xijun Wang,Stanley H. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RVD 框架，将视频动态与视觉上下文解耦为一个紧凑的动态 token，通过语言引导实现视频动态编辑，并支持免训练重定时与外观可控重渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法主要关注改变源视频的外观，对视频动态（时间演变方式）的控制能力有限。作者希望实现对视频动态本身进行直接编辑，同时保持视觉外观不变。

Method: 提出 RVD 框架：1) 通过自监督重建学习一个紧凑、可编辑的动态 token——以第一帧作为视觉上下文，渲染器仅凭动态 token 恢复原始视频，从而迫使 token 编码"场景如何演变"而非"场景长什么样"；2) 设计语言引导的动态 token 编辑器，将源动态转换为目标动态，并采用可扩展的反事实视频对管线与两阶段训练策略进行训练。

Result: 大量实验表明 RVD 能实现有效的视频动态编辑、免训练的重定时（retiming）以及外观可控的重渲染（re-rendering）。

Conclusion: 通过对动态与外观显式解耦并引入语言引导编辑，RVD 显著提升了视频动态的可编辑性与可控性，为视频动态操控提供了一个通用框架。

Abstract: Most video editing methods focus on changing the appearance of the source video, while offering limited control over its dynamics. We introduce Reimagine Video Dynamics (RVD), a framework that disentangles a compact, editable dynamics token from visual context. We learn this token through self-supervised reconstruction: given the first frame as visual context, a renderer must recover the original video from the dynamics token, encouraging it to capture how the scene evolves rather than how it looks. This disentanglement allows video dynamics to be edited directly while preserving visual context. We develop a language-guided dynamics-token editor that transforms source dynamics into target dynamics, and train it with a scalable counterfactual video-pair pipeline and a two-stage training strategy. Extensive experiments show that RVD enables effective video dynamics editing, training-free retiming, and appearance-controlled re-rendering.

</details>


### [40] [Foresight at the Event Boundary: Evaluating Physical Prediction in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36531)
*Estela Monserrat Arriaga Santana,Julian Rosas Scull,Ehécatl Sacamch'en Núñez Rico,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文直接评估视频世界模型的"物理预见"能力：在释放或撞击刚刚发生但后果被截断时，模型能否预测接下来应发生什么。研究构建了基于62段真实自由落体录像、124个片段的"事件锚定"评测集，并将预测分解为后果生成、时间定位与物理实现三个维度，同时开展人类研究作为对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型的评测主要依赖参考相似度、物理定律一致性或判断合理性，只能间接推测模型的预测（anticipation）能力，缺乏对"事件后果预测"这一核心能力（physical foresight）的直接测量。

Method: 提出事件锚定评测协议：使用62段受控真实自由落体录像，生成124个片段，涵盖三种物体类型，配有精细的释放与撞击标注及真实轨迹。协议将后果预测拆解为三个可分别衡量的维度：后果产生（是否生成后续事件）、时间定位（事件是否在正确时机发生）与物理实现（运动是否物理一致）。在六种当代视频生成与世界模型（Runway、Veo、Cosmos-Predict-2.5、MAGI-1 等）上评测，并开展15名参与者、20个条件的人类研究，让参与者从单个事件锚定帧描述预期后果并绘制轨迹。

Result: Runway 和 Veo 生成释放及后续撞击事件的比率超过93%，但往往明显延迟启动；Cosmos-Predict-2.5 与 MAGI-1 则常保持事件前状态，几乎不产生可测量的后果。在可测量下落中，时序合理并不保证运动物理一致。人类预测在总体上偏向真实记录的未来，但不同合理延续之间存在真实模糊性。

Conclusion: 物理预见并非单一能力，而是包含"启动后果、在时间上锚定、实现运动"的递进式挑战序列，当前世界模型在后果生成、时间定位与物理实现三个层面存在各自不同的系统性缺陷，需分别评测与改进。

Abstract: Video world models are largely regarded as predictive models of the physical world and are therefore expected to anticipate the consequences of observed events. However, evaluation has mainly focused on reference similarity, physical-law consistency, or judgment plausibility, estimating anticipation only indirectly. We address this directly: when a release or impact has just occurred but its consequence is withheld, can a world model anticipate what should happen next? We introduce an event-anchored evaluation based on 62 controlled real-world free-fall recordings and 124 clips spanning three object types, with fine-grained release and impact annotations and ground-truth trajectories. The protocol separates consequence production, temporal placement, and physical realization. Across six contemporary video generation and world models, Runway and Veo produce release and subsequent impact events at rates above 93% but often initiate them substantially late, whereas Cosmos-Predict-2.5 and MAGI-1 frequently preserve the pre-event state and produce little or no measurable consequence. Among measurable falls, plausible timing does not necessarily imply physically consistent motion. We further conduct a 15-participant, 20-condition human study in which participants describe the expected consequence from a single event-anchored frame and draw its trajectory. Human predictions favor the recorded future in aggregate while revealing genuine ambiguity among plausible continuations. Overall, physical foresight emerges as a sequence of distinct challenges: initiating a consequence, anchoring it in time, and realizing its motion.

</details>


### [41] [SCCM: Spherically Consistent Coarse Matching for ERP Dense Feature Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36545)
*Gyeonggwan Lee,Eunsoo Im,Seunghwan Hong,Junghun Suh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对等距柱状投影(ERP)全景图像的稠密特征匹配，提出 SCCM 方法，通过在粗匹配阶段引入球面导出的先验（球面位置注意力 SPA 与面积感知共视性 AAC）分别校正拓扑、度量和面积三类畸变，将 Matterport3D 上的 PCK@1° 从 0.229 提升到 0.275。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ERP 是 360° 图像的标准表示，但 ERP 图表引入拓扑、度量和面积三种耦合畸变，导致在平面图像上训练的稠密匹配器在 ERP 上系统性退化；现有标准粗匹配与可见性估计未显式建模这些畸变。

Method: SCCM（球面一致粗匹配）在图表无关的交叉注意力/双 softmax 粗匹配器上叠加两个球面先验：SPA（球面位置注意力）将偏航周期 RoPE（校正拓扑畸变）与切平面偏置（校正度量畸变）配对；AAC（面积感知共视性）在 sigmoid 前施加对数面积校正（校正面积畸变）。以图表无关骨架作为受控参照，分离骨架替换效应与球面先验效应；实例化于 RoMa V1 框架，保持编码器、细化器和损失不变。

Result: 在固定粗骨架、细化器不变条件下，PCK@1° 从 0.229 提升至 0.275；SCCM 在统一 ERP 稠密匹配协议下优于 ERP 原生 EDM（0.163）和 ERP 重训练的 RoMa V1（0.198）；透视训练的匹配器在 ERP 上大多失效；SCCM 零样本迁移至 Stanford2D3D，并在户外 Holo360D 上训练后同样领先。

Conclusion: 在粗阶段接口处校正三类 ERP 畸变能显著提升稠密匹配精度，SCCM 通过球面一致先验在多个基准上取得领先，且无需更换骨架或细化器，验证了显式建模球面畸变对 ERP 稠密匹配的关键作用。

Abstract: Equirectangular projection (ERP) is the standard representation for 360$^\circ$ imagery, and robust dense feature matching on ERP underpins panoramic stereo, view synthesis, and omnidirectional SLAM. Dense matchers trained on flat images degrade systematically on ERP because the chart introduces three coupled distortions -- topological, metric, and area -- that standard coarse matching and visibility estimation do not explicitly model. We show that correcting the three distortions at the coarse-stage interfaces where they arise -- pairwise distortions in attention, per-pixel distortion in covisibility gating -- improves PCK@$1^\circ$ from 0.229 to 0.275 on Matterport3D under a fixed coarse scaffold, with the refiner architecture unchanged -- our central result. Concretely, SCCM (Spherically Consistent Coarse Matching) augments a chart-naive cross-attention/dual-softmax coarse matcher with two sphere-derived priors: Spherical Positional Attention (SPA) pairs a yaw-periodic RoPE (topology) with a tangent-plane bias (metric), and Area-Aware Covisibility (AAC) applies a pre-sigmoid log-area correction (area). The chart-naive scaffold serves as a controlled reference, separating the scaffold-replacement effect from the spherical-prior effect. Instantiated in the RoMa V1 framework with the same frozen encoder, refiner architecture, and loss, SCCM also outperforms the ERP-native EDM (0.163) and an ERP-retrained RoMa V1 (0.198) under a unified ERP dense matching protocol, while perspective-trained matchers largely fail on ERP. It further transfers zero-shot to Stanford2D3D and, when trained on outdoor Holo360D, leads there as well.

</details>


### [42] [How Medical VLMs Underutilize Their Vision Encoders: A Dermatology Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36557)
*Janet Wang,Yunbei Zhang,Xiao Wang,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 医学视觉-语言模型(VLM)存在视觉编码器强但端到端诊断表现弱、且预测缺乏图像证据支撑的问题。本文以皮肤病学为主测试场，系统探究三个假设，通过机制分析发现"先描述后决策"的提示策略可提升视觉注意力30-40%；进一步提出冻结VLM、结合无标签提示与低标签编码器辅助重排序的干预方案，并在五个VLM骨干及多模态上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM的视觉编码器（如MedSigLIP）与完整多模态模型之间存在显著性能差距：在皮肤病学中，即使在零目标标签情况下，MedSigLIP编码器平均比MedGemma高出10.26个百分点，少样本线性探测进一步证明视觉表征本身很强。这说明端到端诊断未能充分利用图像证据，看似合理的预测可能缺乏图像支撑，因此需要探究视觉信息在端到端诊断中的使用方式及其失效原因。

Method: 以皮肤病学为主要测试领域，系统探究三个关于现象成因的假设；对模型内部注意力模式进行机制分析，考察"先描述后决策（describe-then-decide）"提示策略的影响；采用任务特定微调与跨域医学问答评估；并设计冻结VLM的干预方案，即结合无标签提示（label-free prompting）与低标签编码器辅助重排序（encoder-assisted reranking）。

Result: 简单"先描述后决策"提示策略在生成过程中使视觉注意力提升30-40%；任务特定微调改善了皮肤病分类，但在评估中降低了跨域医学问答性能。所提出的冻结VLM、无标签提示加低标签编码器辅助重排序方案有效应对上述挑战，并在五个VLM骨干上得到验证，还在其他医学模态上提供了表示与注意力分析支持。

Conclusion: 医学VLM中视觉信息未被充分利用是端到端诊断性能与视觉编码器能力脱节及预测缺乏图像证据的关键原因。通过改进提示策略与冻结模型的编码器辅助重排序，可在不牺牲跨域能力的前提下提升诊断性能，为医学多模态模型的可靠部署提供了方向。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (VLMs) show significant promise for clinical image understanding, offering accurate diagnosis with interpretable reasoning. However, a critical performance gap exists between their strong vision encoders and the full multimodal model: in dermatology, the MedSigLIP encoder outperforms MedGemma by an average of 10.26 percentage points even when both use zero target-task labels; few-shot linear probing provides further evidence of strong visual representations. This gap motivates an investigation of how visual information is used in end-to-end diagnosis and why plausible-sounding predictions can lack grounding in image evidence. Using dermatology as our primary testbed, we systematically investigate three hypotheses for this phenomenon. We further provide a mechanistic analysis of the model's internal attention patterns, showing that a simple describe-then-decide prompting strategy increases vision attention by 30-40% during generation. Task-specific fine-tuning improves dermatology classification but reduces cross-domain medical question-answering performance in our evaluation. To address these challenges, we combine label-free prompting with low-label encoder-assisted reranking while keeping the VLM frozen. We validate the interventions across five VLM backbones in dermatology and provide supporting representation and attention analyses across additional medical modalities.

</details>


### [43] [FM-ReID: Selective Competitive Token Routing for Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36560)
*Zhiqi Li,Xiaowei Zhou,Zeyuan Sun,Feng Gao,Junyu Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FM-ReID，一种端到端的物体重识别框架，将局部表示学习建模为选择性竞争式 token 路由：通过多个挖掘查询与残差查询竞争 DINOv3 的稠密 token，保留优先分配给各查询的判别性局部信息，并与整体表示联合训练，在动物、行人、车辆 ReID 基准上均取得优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体重识别面临的核心挑战是：不同身份往往具有高度相似的全局外观，而真正区分它们的线索是局部、异构且仅在特定视角下可见的（如动物的斑纹/伤疤、行人的衣物饰品、车辆的局部细节）。视觉基础模型虽已将这些信息编码进稠密 token，但单一的整体描述子会淹没判别性的局部信号，因此需要一种能显式挖掘并组织局部判别信息的方法。

Method: 提出 FM-ReID 端到端框架，其核心是 Competitive Fine-grained Mining（竞争式细粒度挖掘）模块：采用多个挖掘查询（mining queries）与一个残差查询（residual query）对 DINOv3 的稠密 token 进行竞争性分配；通过 above-prior selection 保留优先分配给各挖掘查询的 token 构成多查询局部描述子，残差槽（residual slot）则接收被排除在检索描述子之外、不具判别性的 token。最终将多查询描述子与整体（holistic）表示联合训练用于检索，无需固定空间划分或等面积约束。

Result: FM-ReID 在动物、行人、车辆三类 ReID 基准上均取得了强有力的结果，验证了竞争式 token 路由能够有效增强基于视觉基础模型的整体表示。

Conclusion: 竞争式 token 路由是增强整体基础模型表示的有效途径：通过让多个查询竞争式地捕获稠密 token 中的局部判别信息，并结合整体表示联合训练，可以在不依赖固定空间分区的前提下显著提升物体重识别性能。

Abstract: Object re-identification (ReID) faces a recurring challenge: different identities can share highly similar global appearances, while the cues that distinguish them are localized, heterogeneous, and visible only under particular viewpoints. This challenge arises in animal ReID through markings, contours, and scars, in person ReID through subtle clothing and accessory cues, and in vehicle ReID through localized appearance details. Although visual foundation models encode such information in dense tokens, a single holistic descriptor can obscure discriminative local signals. We propose FM-ReID, an end-to-end framework that formulates local representation learning as selective competitive token routing. Its Competitive Fine-grained Mining module uses multiple mining queries and a residual query to compete for dense DINOv3 tokens. Above-prior selection retains tokens preferentially allocated to each mining query, while the residual slot receives tokens excluded from the retrieval descriptors. The resulting multi-query descriptors are jointly trained with a holistic representation for retrieval, without fixed spatial partitions or equal-area constraints. FM-ReID achieves strong results on animal, person, and vehicle ReID benchmarks, supporting competitive token routing as an effective way to augment holistic foundation-model representations.

</details>


### [44] [ThinkingGuard: Decoding Implicit Hazards via Step-by-Step Risk Attribution in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36562)
*Ruochen Zhang,Yao Huang,Yitong Sun,Jiahe Xie,Jin Yan,Jifan Ma,Yuanfang Guo,Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态大模型（MLLM）在安全关键场景中的“多模态隐式风险”——单个安全的文本与中性视觉实体在逻辑上结合后诱导不安全输出——本文构建了首个显式建模风险组合性的数据集 TriggerBench，并提出基于情境意识理论、采用步骤监督结构化推理与步骤奖励蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）训练的护栏模型 ThinkingGuard。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法忽视了跨模态风险激活的底层机制，导致单模态捷径学习和幻觉式解释，无法有效应对“文本与图像各自无害、组合后产生风险”的隐式威胁；同时缺乏能提供严格训练与细粒度评估基础的数据集。

Method: 首先构建 TriggerBench（5,600 实例），通过形式化分离关键元素（Key Elements）与触发元素（Trigger Elements）构造反事实对比对，消除风险残留并迫使模型进行真实逻辑推理；进而提出步骤监督结构化推理训练框架，借鉴情境意识理论将隐式风险识别解耦为渐进认知阶段，并用步骤奖励蒙特卡洛树搜索探索最优推理轨迹，再通过双重约束偏好对齐（Dual-Constraint Preference Alignment）蒸馏到模型中，训练得到专门护栏模型 ThinkingGuard。

Result: 在标准安全基准与隐式安全基准上的大量实验表明，ThinkingGuard 取得了强有力的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模风险组合性的 TriggerBench 与基于结构化推理的 ThinkingGuard，有效缓解了 MLLM 的多模态隐式风险，提升了安全关键场景下的可靠性。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains, their reliability is threatened by multimodal implicit risks. Unlike explicit threats, these hazards emerge when individually benign text and neutral visual entities logically converge to induce unsafe outputs. Current detection methods fail to address this because they overlook the underlying risk activation mechanisms that govern cross-modal risk activation, leading to single-modality shortcut learning and hallucinated rationalizations. To bridge this gap, we first construct TriggerBench, the first dataset explicitly modeling risk compositionality (5,600 instances). By formally isolating Key Elements and Trigger Elements to build counterfactual contrastive pairs, TriggerBench eliminates risk residues and forces models to perform genuine logical deduction rather than superficial pattern matching, which provides a rigorous foundation for both large-scale training and fine-grained evaluation. Building on this, we propose a Step-Supervised Structured Reasoning training framework and employ it to train ThinkingGuard, a specialized guard model. Inspired by Situation Awareness theory, we decouple implicit risk identification into progressive cognitive stages, and utilize a step-reward Monte Carlo Tree Search algorithm to explore optimal reasoning trajectories, which are then distilled into the model through Dual-Constraint Preference Alignment. Extensive experiments across both standard and implicit safety benchmarks demonstrate that ThinkingGuard achieves strong performance. Project resources are available at https://github.com/FroggyChen/ThinkingGuard.

</details>


### [45] [AffectReveal: Event-Grounded Emotion Recognition Beyond Visual Appearances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36563)
*Yihao Qian,Runhao Zeng,Sicheng Zhao,Feng Liang,Hongmin Cai,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉情感识别通常假设仅凭图像/视频即可完成预测，但同一可见反应可能对应不同情感。本文提出事件扎根的情感识别（EGER），构建基准 EGER-Bench，并设计无需调优的 AffectReveal 框架，通过恢复并交叉验证影响情感的事件，显著提升多种模型在多个设置下的情感识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉情感识别忽略了图像/视频之外的事件上下文，导致同一可见反应（如流泪可能是悲伤或喜悦）被误判。作者认为情感识别必须恢复决定情感的事件，而仅靠语义相关性不足以解决该问题，因为事件的实体、焦点人物角色、关系或结果被误解时可能推出错误情感。

Method: 提出 Event-Grounded Emotion Recognition (EGER) 任务，并构建 EGER-Bench（10,052 个视频和 10,734 张图像，覆盖 11 种情感、两个来源域、四种视觉设置）。进一步提出 AffectReveal 框架：无需调优，先在这些影响情感的关键因素上构建并独立验证有证据支撑的备选事件，再通过双向原子证据支持将恢复的事件与去脸（face-masked）的媒体内事实进行交叉核对，同时保留原始未掩码输入用于最终预测。

Result: 六名标注者的研究表明，事件上下文将人类识别准确率从 33.96% 提升到 72.08%，证实仅靠视觉证据通常不足。在三个下游模型和四种输入设置上，AffectReveal 平均带来 5.26--10.53 个百分点的 UAR 提升；对三个可微调模型，未调优的 AffectReveal 模型在全部 12 项准确率比较中均超过仅视觉微调的对应模型，且无需更新下游参数。

Conclusion: 情感识别需要超越可见输入、恢复影响情感的事件。EGER-Bench 揭示了视觉证据不足的问题，AffectReveal 通过在影响情感的关键因素上验证事件并与媒体内事实双向交叉核对，无需调参即可稳健提升下游情感识别性能，显示出事件扎根方法对视觉情感理解的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: Visual emotion recognition commonly assumes that all evidence required for prediction is contained in the observed image or video. Yet the same visible reaction can convey different emotions depending on events beyond the input: tears, for example, may indicate grief or joy. We formulate Event-Grounded Emotion Recognition (EGER), where emotion recognition requires recovering the affect-determining event. We construct EGER-Bench, comprising 10,052 videos and 10,734 images across 11 emotions, two source domains, and four visual settings. A study with six annotators shows that event context raises human recognition accuracy from 33.96% to 72.08%, confirming that visual evidence alone is often insufficient. Semantic relevance alone does not solve EGER: a plausible event may imply the wrong emotion if its identity, focal-person role, relationship, or outcome is misinterpreted. We therefore propose AffectReveal, a tuning-free framework that first constructs and independently verifies evidence-grounded alternatives over these affect-critical factors. It then cross-checks the recovered event against face-masked in-media facts through bidirectional atomic evidence support, while retaining the original unmasked input for final prediction. Across three downstream models and four input settings, AffectReveal yields average UAR gains of 5.26--10.53 points. For three fine-tunable models, it also enables untuned models to outperform their fine-tuned visual-only counterparts in all 12 accuracy comparisons, without updating downstream parameters.

</details>


### [46] [Beyond Legibility: Benchmarking Visual Text Rendering and In-Place Editing in Unified Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36598)
*Ziying Zhang,Litao Li,Junchao Liao,Tianyi Zeng,Siyu Zhu,Long Qin,Zhenghao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VidScribe，一个面向视频中视觉文本生成的统一诊断基准，覆盖 T2V、R2V、I2V、V2V 四种生成任务，揭示视频文本能力并非单一维度、任务高度不对称，并可作为可操作的训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型能呈现逼真运动与光影，但视觉文本一旦生成即容易失败；与传统场景内容不同，视觉文本对笔画损坏、时序不稳定或编辑错误极其敏感，会立即破坏可读性与真实性。而现有基准要么把文本视为附带内容，要么使用忽略时序动态的静态 OCR 指标，无法系统评估这一关键能力。

Method: 构建 VidScribe 基准：包含 803 个人工核验样本，覆盖四类生成机制（T2V 语言到视频、R2V 参考文本转写、I2V 动态下维持文本、V2V 局部文本编辑），并横跨一个 12 轴条件正交因子空间（涵盖内在文本属性、物理成像条件、时序行为）。评估采用 track-grounded 门控套件，在严格可测性条件下使用 11 个共享指标和 2 个任务专属探针，对 11 个商用与开源系统进行基准测试；此外利用基准对齐的偏好优化作为可操作的训练信号。

Result: 视频文本能力非单一整体：内容识别与笔画级字形正确性相互解耦；任务高度不对称，I2V 维持文本最可靠，V2V 编辑是主要瓶颈；性能退化主要集中在少数文本中心的结构与时序因素上，而非恶劣成像条件；视觉参考提升的是字形与排版保真度而非内容准确性，局部编辑无法在不破坏未声明源文本的情况下隔离目标文本；基准对齐的偏好优化可显著改善视觉文本生成。

Conclusion: VidScribe 为视频视觉文本生成提供了首个统一、细粒度、时序感知的诊断基准，揭示现有系统在文本生成能力上的非单一性与任务不对称性，定位了关键退化因素，并证明该基准能为模型训练提供可操作信号，推动视频文本生成能力的提升。

Abstract: A video can exhibit convincing motion and photorealism yet fail immediately when visual text collapses. Unlike generic scene content, visual text is unforgiving in video generation: minor stroke corruption, temporal instability, or editing errors instantly break legibility and realism. Existing benchmarks overlook this challenge by treating text as incidental or using static OCR metrics that ignore temporal dynamics. We introduce VidScribe, a unified diagnostic benchmark spanning four generation regimes: writing from language (T2V), transferring text identity from a reference (R2V), sustaining text under dynamics (I2V), and localized text editing (V2V). VidScribe contains 803 human-verified samples across a 12-axis conditionally orthogonal factor space covering Intrinsic Text Properties, Physical Imaging Conditions, and Temporal Behavior. For reliable evaluation, we build a track-grounded, gated suite with 11 shared metrics and 2 task-specific probes under strict measurability conditions. Benchmarking 11 commercial and open-source systems shows that video text capability is non-monolithic, with content recognition decoupled from stroke-level glyph correctness. Performance is highly task-asymmetric: I2V sustains text most reliably, whereas V2V editing is the primary bottleneck. Counter-intuitively, degradation concentrates on a small subset of text-centric structural and temporal factors rather than adverse imaging conditions. Further probes show that visual references improve glyph and typographic fidelity rather than content accuracy, while localized editing fails to isolate target text without corrupting undeclared source text. Beyond evaluation, VidScribe also provides an actionable training signal, where benchmark-aligned preference optimization measurably improves visual text generation. https://huggingface.co/datasets/Vicky0720/VidScribe.

</details>


### [47] [Scaling Video Generation for Reasoning: At What Cost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36599)
*Weihang Guo,Xiaoyu Wu,Yifei Wang,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视频生成的规模扩展是否能让模型推理出过去帧中隐藏的信息及其计算代价，使用一个受控的 2x2x2 魔方基准任务，要求从固定视角预测九步规定转动后的魔方状态。研究发现模型早期即学会合理的魔方几何，但要正确预测贴纸配置需要大量训练；更低的 MSE 损失并不总是对应更强的下游推理能力，小模型在有限算力下状态准确率更高，而大模型需要更多训练才能反超，且符号化状态监督能显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视频生成模型的规模扩展（scaling）是否有助于对隐藏信息进行推理（即从可见面推断不可见面状态），以及这种能力需要多少计算量才能获得，从而厘清损失下降与实际推理能力之间的关系。

Method: 构建一个受控的 2x2x2 魔方基准：从已复原的魔方出发，规定九步转动，模型需从固定视角观察三个可见面并预测每步后的魔方状态。使用精确的模拟器提供标签，并以验证 MSE、贴纸配置准确率及状态准确率评估不同参数量（如 20M、70M、1B）的自回归模型在缩放数据与算力下的表现，同时对比有无符号化状态监督的训练方式。

Result: 模型早期学会合理的魔方几何，但正确预测贴纸配置需要更大量的训练；验证 MSE 近似幂律缩放，但低 MSE 不保证强推理能力。约 0.1 PF-days 时，70M 模型在 44.6% 的后动作帧中正确预测可见贴纸配置，而 1B 模型仅 0.3%；1B 模型在 3.14 PF-days 时达到 83.7%。在相同训练数据预算下，符号化状态监督将 20M 模型的帧准确率从 31.1% 提升到 67.3%。

Conclusion: 视频生成模型的规模扩展确实能逐步获得对隐藏状态的推理能力，但需要大量训练。评估视频模型时不能仅依赖 MSE 损失，因为损失下降与下游推理能力并不一致；在计算资源有限时小模型可能更优。此外，将符号化状态变化作为监督信号引入可以显著提升推理准确率，是对单纯靠规模扩展的一种有效补充。

Abstract: We study whether scaling video generation enables models to reason about hidden information from the past frames, and at what computational cost. Our controlled benchmark requires predicting nine prescribed moves of an initially solved 2x2x2 Rubik's Cube from a fixed view of three faces. Correct predictions require inferring how actions change hidden states, and the simulator provides exact ground truth for evaluation. Models learn plausible cube geometry early, while correct sticker configurations require substantially more training. Although validation MSE follows approximate power-law scaling, lower MSE loss does not reliably indicate downstream reasoning capabilities. Smaller autoregressive models achieve higher state accuracy with limited compute, while larger models reach higher accuracy after more training. At roughly 0.1 PF-days, the 70M-parameter model correctly predicts the visible sticker configuration in 44.6% of post-action frames, compared with 0.3% for the 1B model, which reaches 83.7% at 3.14 PF-days. Symbolic state supervision raises the 20M model's frame accuracy from 31.1% to 67.3% at the same training-data budget, suggesting that learning representations of state changes can complement scaling.

</details>

### [48] [FD-VAD: Semantic Endpoint Detection for Streaming Full-Duplex Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35791)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FD-VAD 是一种无需 ASR 的流式语义端点检测器，直接由因果音频窗口预测 Continue/Stop，在 TurnBench 上以零样本方式获得最高 EOT 召回率 0.853（FP≤0.10）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音交互中的自然轮流说话需要从部分语音判断停顿是犹豫还是完整对话意图的结束。传统声学 VAD 缺乏语义信息，而级联 ASR 端点检测依赖转录并增加处理阶段。作者旨在直接将语义端点检测建模为因果音频-语言推理任务，摆脱 ASR 与对话状态跟踪的依赖。

Method: 提出 FD-VAD，将语义端点检测形式化为一个因果音频-语言推理任务：将有界因果音频窗口直接映射为 Continue/Stop 决策。架构上组合冻结的语音编码器、轻量模态适配器，以及参数高效微调（parameter-efficiently adapted）的语言模型，并采用 last-chunk 训练目标以支持流式推理。此外引入两个关键机制：置信度门控的端点承诺（confidence-gated endpoint commitment）用于权衡打断与延迟；边界聚焦的难负样本采样（boundary-focused hard-negative sampling）以提升歧义轮次边界附近的决策能力。

Result: 在域内与对话评测中，FD-VAD 超越了强流式与非流式语义轮次分类器，并在 TurnBench dev 集上以零样本设置获得合格系统中最高的 EOT 召回率 0.853（FP≤0.10）。

Conclusion: 实验证明语义端点检测可直接从流式音频完成，无需中间的 ASR 或对话状态跟踪，为全双工语音交互提供了更简洁高效的端点检测方案。

Abstract: Natural turn-taking in full-duplex voice interaction requires determining from partial speech whether a pause reflects hesitation or a completed conversational intent. Acoustic voice activity detection lacks this semantic information, while cascaded ASR-based endpointing introduces transcription dependence and additional processing stages. We formulate semantic endpoint detection as a causal audio-language reasoning task and introduce FD-VAD, an ASR-free streaming endpointer that maps bounded causal audio windows directly to Continue/Stop decisions. FD-VAD combines a frozen speech encoder with a lightweight modality adapter and a parameter-efficiently adapted language model, using a last-chunk training objective for streaming inference. We further introduce confidence-gated endpoint commitment to control interruption versus delay and boundary-focused hard-negative sampling to improve decisions around ambiguous turn boundaries. Across in-domain and conversational evaluations, FD-VAD outperforms strong streaming and non-streaming semantic turn classifiers, and achieves the highest EOT recall among qualifying systems on TurnBench dev set $0.853$ (at FP<=0.10) in a zero-shot setting. These results show that semantic endpointing can be performed directly from streaming audio without intermediate ASR or dialogue state tracking.

</details>


### [49] [Sieve and Sage: Efficient Distraction Filtering for Reliable RALM Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35794)
*Jongbin Won,Sung Geun An,Jay-yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将检索增强语言模型（RALM）的检索失败分解为"不可回答"与"干扰"两种状态，提出 Sieve（轻量筛选模块）+ Sage（LLM 生成与弃答）的两阶段框架，在准确率、Macro-F1 和推理速度上均显著优于单阶段基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RALM 弃答方法依赖单一 LLM 一步处理异构检索失败，弃答性能有限且计算成本高。作者借鉴苏格拉底"知无知"的理念，认为模型应在检索证据无法支撑可靠回答时学会弃答，并将失败细分为证据缺失（不可回答）与证据混杂（干扰）两类，分别处理以提升效率与可靠性。

Method: 提出 Sieve 和 Sage 两阶段框架：先由轻量模块 Sieve 对检索到的文档集合进行筛选，预先检测并剔除冲突、否定或对抗性的干扰证据；仅在通过筛选后，才调用高成本的 LLM（Sage）进行有根据的生成与弃答判断，从而在保证可靠性的同时降低计算开销。

Result: 在通用领域和高风险专家领域评估中，Sieve 与 Sage 框架能预先检测干扰噪声，相较单阶段基线最高将系统准确率提升 69.4 个百分点、Macro-F1 提升 55.2 个百分点，并实现最高 1.99 倍的加速。

Conclusion: 该框架通过"轻量筛选 + 高成本生成"的分解式设计，构建了一个高效且可靠的、带弃答能力的 RALM 流程，在性能与计算效率两方面均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Just as Socrates recognized the limits of his own knowledge, Retrieval-Augmented Language Models (RALMs) should learn to abstain when the retrieved evidence cannot support a reliable response. Existing approaches largely rely on monolithic LLMs to handle heterogeneous retrieval failures in a single step, resulting in limited abstention performance and high computational costs. We instead decompose retrieval failures into two distinct states: (i) the unanswerable state, where the required evidence is absent, and (ii) the distracted state, where relevant evidence is mixed with conflicting, negated, or adversarial information. Based on this decomposition, we introduce a lightweight module (Sieve) that screens retrieved document sets for distracting evidence before invoking a costly LLM (Sage) for grounded generation and abstention. Evaluated across both general and high-stakes expert domains, our Sieve and Sage framework preemptively detects distracting noise, improving system accuracy by up to 69.4 percentage points and Macro-F1 by 55.2 percentage points compared to one-stage baselines. Furthermore, it achieves up to a 1.99x speedup, establishing a highly efficient and reliable abstention pipeline for RALM with abstention.

</details>


### [50] [Developing an OCR model for Extracting Information from Invoices with Korean Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35796)
*Xiem HoangVan,Phu TranQuang,Minh DinhBao,Tien VuHuu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向韩语发票的高效光学字符识别（OCR）模型，将深度学习与图像预处理技术相结合，在自建发票数据集上取得了87%的F1分数，且处理时间可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发票是包含购买物品、时间、金额等多种信息的商业文档，自动提取其中的关键信息至关重要。韩语约有8000万母语使用者，不仅在朝韩两国，在越南、菲律宾等韩国企业众多的国家也发挥着重要作用。因此，需要一种能够自动、准确地从韩语发票中提取信息的OCR解决方案。

Method: 提出一种将深度学习模型与多种图像预处理技术相结合的光学字符识别（OCR）模型，用于从韩语发票中自动提取信息。

Result: 在一组采集的丰富发票数据集上评估，所提出的OCR模型取得了87%的F1分数，并且处理时间开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 结合深度学习与图像预处理的OCR模型能够高效、准确地提取韩语发票中的关键信息，在精度与处理速度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Invoices are commercial documents that contain various pieces of information, including the purchased items, time, and total money. Making the extraction of important information crucial. The stored information serves different purposes. Korean language is the native language of about 80 million people, playing an important role in not only South and North Korea but also in many other countries such as Vietnam, Philippine where a large number of Korean companies are located. In this context, to automatically extract proper information from the invoices with Korean language, we propose an efficient Optical Character Recognition (OCR) model in which a deep learning model is combined with some image preprocessing techniques. The proposed OCR model is assessed in a rich set of collected invoices showing that 87% F1-score can be achieved with negligible time processing.

</details>


### [51] [Evaluating the Effects of Prompt Perturbation on Bias and Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35804)
*Mamehgol Yousefi,Ahmad Shahi,Mos Sharifi,Alvaro Romera,Simon Hoermann,Tham Piumsomboon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估大语言模型在决策任务中对原始问题扰动变体的鲁棒性，发现扰动在某些模型上可减轻偏见与幻觉，且 Claude 3 表现优于 GPT3.5 等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为智能助手被广泛部署于决策场景，但其复杂性带来的偏见与幻觉问题引发对其可靠性的担忧，因此需要系统评估模型对问题扰动的鲁棒性。

Method: 通过对原始查询施加扰动生成变体，在多个决策任务数据集上比较不同 LLM（如 Claude 3、GPT3.5）在扰动条件下的表现，评估其偏见与幻觉的缓解程度及任务适配性。

Result: 与先前研究相反，扰动在某些 LLM 上能够减轻偏见和幻觉；Claude 3 在多数数据集代表的任务上更有效，GPT3.5 等模型表现参差不齐，部分场景可比、部分场景显著落后。

Conclusion: 研究提示部署 LLM 辅助决策工具前必须进行严格测试与验证，以确保可靠性与有效性；该工作为更稳健可信的决策型 AI 助手开发提供了见解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in various natural language processing tasks, leading to their widespread deployment as intelligent assistants in decision-making contexts. However, the increasing complexity of these models raises concerns about their reliability, particularly regarding bias and hallucination. In this work, we evaluate the robustness of LLMs to perturbed variations of the original inquiry in decision-making tasks. We show that contrary to previous studies, perturbations can mitigate bias and hallucination in some LLMs over other models. It's found that Claude 3 is more effective for the tasks represented in most datasets, whereas models like GPT3.5 exhibit varying levels of adequacy, performing comparably in some cases but falling significantly behind in others. These insights are crucial for understanding the practical implications of deploying LLM-based assistants as effective decision-support tools in real-world applications, emphasising the need for rigorous testing and validation to ensure reliability and effectiveness. This study contributes to the growing body of research on LLM evaluation and provides insights for developing more robust and trustworthy AI assistants in critical decision-making contexts.

</details>


### [52] [Alignment Forecasting: Predicting Misalignment From Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35805)
*Chen Yueh-Han,Bruce W. Lee,Ilia Sucholutsky,Tomek Korbak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"对齐预测"（Alignment Forecasting）任务，即在实际训练前预测微调是否会引发对齐失败，并构建了包含超5000个预测问题的基准 ALIGNMENTFORECASTBENCH。作者设计了一种预测脚手架（LLM 读取数据集并评分 + 简单学习模型），其表现优于直接提示的前沿模型和微调模型，并可辅助数据筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法只能通过训练后审计（post-hoc audits）发现对齐失败，效率低、成本高；仅凭肉眼检查数据往往无法判断模型是否会变得广泛失对齐。因此需要一种在训练前就能预测对齐失败的方法，以补充事后审计。

Method: 定义 Alignment Forecasting 任务：给定目标模型、微调数据集和失败模式（如欺骗、谄媚），预测微调后该失败模式是否显著增加。构建 ALIGNMENTFORECASTBENCH（17 个目标模型、32 个数据集、16 种失败模式、超 5000 个问题）。提出预测脚手架：先由 LLM 读取数据集并对其"如何强而广地推动模型失当行为"进行评分，再用一个简单的学习模型将该评分与失败模式的基准率、目标模型的先验倾向相结合，输出预测概率。此外，利用其信号标记出前沿模型分类器漏掉的坏训练样本，并在 UltraChat 等真实后训练数据上做过滤实验验证。

Result: 前沿模型直接提示在 ALIGNMENTFORECASTBENCH 上表现差；所提预测脚手架预测准确度显著高于随机水平，且优于在此任务上微调的模型，也优于允许观察较弱模型微调后行为的简单预测器。该脚手架能标记出前沿模型分类器遗漏的坏样本；在多数情况下，从 UltraChat 中过滤这些样本可使模型在多项选择评测中更对齐，但在开放式对话中的收益尚不明确。

Conclusion: 在 SFT（监督微调）设置下，训练前预测多种对齐失败是可行的，但目前还不足以可靠指导实际训练数据的筛选，仍需更多进展。对齐预测有望成为事后审计的有力补充。

Abstract: Training a language model on data with a narrow flaw can sometimes make the model broadly misaligned. Inspecting the data at face value often does not settle whether it will emerge, and today it is caught only after training, by auditing the resulting model. To complement post-hoc audits, we introduce Alignment Forecasting: the task of predicting alignment failures before training. Given a target model, a fine-tuning dataset, and a failure mode such as deception or sycophancy, a forecaster outputs the probability that fine-tuning would meaningfully increase that failure mode. To measure progress on alignment forecasting, we introduce ALIGNMENTFORECASTBENCH, a benchmark of over 5,000 forecasting questions spanning 17 target models, 32 datasets, and 16 failure modes. Frontier models prompted directly perform poorly on ALIGNMENTFORECASTBENCH. We therefore propose a forecasting scaffold in which an LLM reads the dataset and rates how strongly and broadly it pushes the model toward misbehavior, and a simple learned model combines that rating with the failure mode's base rate and the target model's prior tendency. This forecasts well above chance, and beats a model fine-tuned on the task and a simple forecaster allowed to see how weaker models behaved after fine-tuning on the same data. Its signals also flag problematic training examples that a frontier-model classifier misses. Filtering those examples out from real post-training data such as UltraChat results in more aligned models on our multiple-choice evaluation in most cases, though the benefit in open-ended conversations is unclear. More progress is needed before forecasts can reliably guide training data curation in practice, but our results suggest that forecasting many alignment failures before training can be tractable in the SFT setting.

</details>


### [53] [From Lexical Baselines to Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Structured Skill and Responsibility-Level Extraction with the SFIA Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35806)
*Ranuga Disansa,U. S. Samarasinghe,Lasith Gunawardena*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将SFIA框架下的技能提取形式化为"从自由文本中结构化预测(skill, level)对"的任务，系统比较了五种策略（词法基线、稠密检索+LLM重排、零样本schema约束LLM、单智能体RAG、三智能体crew），并公开了一个由自动化agentic流程构建的SFIA 9语料库。结果发现：检索式匹配召回技能最多但生成式策略精度更高；只有显式决策层级的策略才能可靠预测层级；增加agent角色（crew）仅加倍延迟而未提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能提取系统多将技能表示为无层级信息的扁平标签，忽略了技能被实践时所在的"职责层级"这一关键维度；SFIA框架恰好定义了147项专业技能及七个职责层级，但尚无可复现的、针对SFIA的LLM自动化提取方案，导致无法支撑精细化的人力规划。

Method: 将任务形式化为对自由文本做(skill, level)结构化预测，并围绕SFIA封闭词表提出三个研究问题。构建并公开一个完全由自动化agentic流程生成的SFIA 9语料库。在专家标注的欧洲ICT岗位画像上，系统比较五种策略：①词法基线；②稠密检索+LLM重排；③零样本schema约束LLM；④单智能体agentic RAG；⑤三智能体"检索者–匹配者–验证者"crew。从技能识别准确率、层级预测可靠性、agentic设计增益三个维度进行评估。

Result: ①检索式匹配识别出的技能数量最多，而生成式策略在精度上显著更高；②只有将层级作为显式决策来分配的策略才能可靠预测层级，基于相似度选择的方式准确率差两倍以上；③三智能体crew相较于单智能体方案将延迟翻倍但未带来精度提升，说明在封闭分类法匹配任务中单纯增加agent角色并不自动带来收益。

Conclusion: 该工作为面向SFIA的、层级感知的结构化技能提取提供了首个可复现基准，揭示了检索与生成策略在召回率/精度上的互补性、显式层级决策对可靠性的必要性，以及agent多角色设计在封闭词表匹配场景下的无增益现象，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Automated skill extraction underpins workforce planning, yet most systems represent skills as flat labels with no notion of the responsibility level at which a skill is practiced. The Skills Framework for the Information Age (SFIA) captures exactly this dimension, defining 147 professional skills across seven responsibility levels, but no automated LLM-based extraction targeting SFIA has been reported. We formalize the task as structured prediction of (skill, level) pairs from free text and ask three questions: how accurately can text be mapped onto SFIA's closed vocabulary, which strategies reliably predict the level alongside the skill, and do agentic designs improve on simpler retrieval and prompting? We evaluate five strategies (a lexical baseline, dense retrieval with LLM reranking, a zero-shot schema-constrained LLM, single-agent agentic RAG, and a three-agent retriever--matcher--verifier crew) against expert-mapped European ICT role profiles, all drawing on an SFIA~9 corpus built by a fully automated agentic pipeline that we release. Retrieval-based matching identifies the most skills while generative strategies are markedly more precise; only strategies assigning the level as an explicit decision predict it reliably, with similarity-based selection more than twice as inaccurate; and the crew doubles latency without improving accuracy, so added agent roles do not automatically benefit closed-taxonomy matching. These results provide the first reproducible baseline for structured, level-aware skill extraction against SFIA.

</details>


### [54] [Environment Steering: Using Data Flow Control to Improve Agent Utility and Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35807)
*Charlie Summers,Prajwal Raghunath,Aaditya Pai,Mayur Kulkarni,Zhuo Zhang,Oliver Kennedy,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"环境引导"（Environment Steering）防御方法：在执行环境中对 LLM 智能体的工具调用进行运行时安全检查，违规时引导智能体转向安全轨迹，既能阻止攻击又能帮助恢复，优于现有的前置约束、输入/输出修改和 LLM 评判等方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方式存在局限：执行前约束依赖模型行为、修改工具输入/输出或依赖 LLM 评判器，它们要么无法保证安全，要么直接阻断不安全动作而不帮助智能体恢复，导致任务无法继续完成。作者认为安全应由执行环境在智能体运行过程中强制执行，并在违规时引导智能体转向安全的替代方案。

Method: 将智能体与 harness 的执行状态建模为数据库表，跟踪记录级别的数据流，并在运行时将这些数据流与声明式安全策略进行比对检查。一旦检测到违规，便生成策略相关且上下文相关的反馈，引导智能体转向安全的执行轨迹。

Result: 在 AgentDyn 评测集上，相比无防御的基线，该方法在保持 0% 攻击成功率的同时，提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 环境引导是一种有效的运行时安全防御机制，能够在保证零攻击成功率的前提下提升任务完成率，表明由执行环境主动强制执行安全并引导智能体恢复是比传统方法更优的路径。

Abstract: LLM agents can make unsafe tool calls even when instructed to behave safely. Existing defenses constrain agents before execution, modify tool inputs/outputs, or rely on LLM judges; these approaches may depend on model behavior or block unsafe actions without helping the agent recover. We argue that the execution environment should instead enforce safety as the agent runs and steer it toward safe alternatives when violations occur---we call this Environment Steering. We implement this by modeling the agent and harness execution state as database tables, track the record-level data flows, and check these data flows against declarative policies during runtime. When violations are detected, policy- and context-specific feedback steers the agent toward safe trajectories. On AgentDyn, this enables the agent to improve task success rate over no-defense while achieving 0% attack success rate.

</details>


### [55] [When Successful Memories Mislead Embodied Agents:Memory Adaption For Task-Conditioned Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35808)
*Quanquan Li,Hongbo Zhang,Yihe Chi,Liuyang Song,Jingyu Li,Yuxiang Huang,Hongzhen Zhang,Guitao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MATE，一种位于检索与执行之间的确定性记忆适配流程，将历史轨迹转化为执行导向记忆，包括去除过时控制上下文、提取条件-动作-效应转移、经验证的动作归一化、任务依赖表示选择及固定预算内序列化，无需额外LLM推理。实验表明MATE在134个ALFWorld任务上以约十分之一的token实现81.3%（Qwen2.5-14B）与93.3%（Qwen2.5-72B）的成功率，且动作归一化是恢复检索经验效用的核心机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体中经验复用可减少重复探索，但既往成功的轨迹未必适应当前执行上下文。现有记忆系统主要优化构建与检索，语义相关性与历史成功率不足以保证效用，因为检索到的经验可能包含不兼容的动作或结构层级不当，因此需要在检索与执行之间引入独立的记忆适配环节。

Method: 提出MATE，一种确定性的检索后处理流程，包含五个步骤：(1)移除过时的控制上下文；(2)提取条件-动作-效应(C-A-E)转移；(3)应用经过验证的动作归一化；(4)选择任务依赖的表示形式；(5)在固定预算下序列化结果。整个过程不引入额外的LLM推理。

Result: 在134个ALFWorld任务上，MATE在Qwen2.5-14B上取得81.3%任务成功率，在Qwen2.5-72B上取得93.3%成功率，同时仅使用原始轨迹约十分之一的token。控制对比实验表明，经验证的动作归一化是MATE恢复检索经验效用的主要机制，证明记忆适配是检索与具身执行之间的独立阶段。

Conclusion: MATE证明通过确定性记忆适配可以将检索到的历史经验转化为执行导向记忆，显著提升任务成功率并大幅降低token开销。研究支持将记忆适配视为检索与具身执行之间的独立处理阶段，其中动作归一化起主导作用。

Abstract: Experience reuse can reduce repeated exploration in embodied agents, but a trajectory that succeeded previously may be unsuitable for the current execution context. Existing memory systems pri marily optimize construction and retrieval; semantic relevance and historical success therefore remain insufficient when retrieved ex perience contains incompatible actions or an inappropriate level of structure. We introduce Memory Adaptation for Task-Conditioned Execution (MATE), a deterministic post-retrieval procedure that converts trajectories into execution-oriented memory. MATE re moves obsolete control context, extracts condition-action-effect transitions, applies verified action normalization, selects a task dependent representation, and serializes the result under a fixed budget without additional LLM inference. On 134 ALFWorld tasks, MATE achieves task success rates of 81.3% and 93.3% with Qwen2.5-14B and 72B while using approximately one-tenth of the tokens required by raw trajectories. Controlled comparisons show that verified action normalization is the principal mechanism by which MATE restores the utility of retrieved experience, support ing memory adaptation as a distinct stage between retrieval and embodied execution.

</details>


### [56] [Can Multimodal Large Language Models Generate and Detect Multimodal Social Media Fake News?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35809)
*Jiyao Yang,Yang Liu,Zhenyue Qin,Qingyu Chen,Xiuzhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探究多模态大模型（MLLMs）能否被用来制造逼真的多模态假新闻、以及它们能否可靠检测此类假新闻，通过多智能体框架生成9000余条成对的多模态新闻并基准测试16个模型，发现多数模型远未达到人类水平的检测准确率，尤其在图像真实性识别上失败严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展引发了对多模态大模型被滥用于社交媒体大规模虚假信息传播的担忧；现有研究多聚焦文本虚假信息，但MLLMs能否被利用来捏造逼真的多模态假新闻、以及它们能否可靠地检测出这类假新闻，这一根本问题仍未得到解答。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架，由故事智能体（story agent）、图像智能体（image agent）和评论智能体（critic agent）协同工作，生成能够貌似合理地反驳真实新闻的社交媒体帖子；该框架被用于在科学、健康、娱乐三个领域生成超过9000对成对的多模态新闻帖子，并对16个开源与闭源MLLMs进行自动检测基准测试。

Result: 大多数模型在检测准确率上远低于人类水平，并且在判断图像真实性（image authenticity）上出现严重失败；研究为构建针对社交媒体假新闻的鲁棒防御提供了基础。

Conclusion: MLLMs可以被利用来制造逼真的多模态假新闻，而现有MLLMs在自动检测这类内容方面能力不足，尤其在图像真实性判别上存在关键缺陷；该研究为开发更强大的社交媒体假新闻防御机制奠定了基础，代码与数据已开源发布。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative AI raises concerns about the misuse of Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) for large-scale disinformation campaigns on social media. Despite existing research on textual disinformation, a fundamental question remains unanswered: can MLLMs be exploited to fabricate realistic multimodal fake news, and can they reliably detect it? We introduce a multi-agent framework in which a story agent, an image agent, and a critic agent collaborate to produce fake social media posts that plausibly counter true news. We apply the framework to generate over 9,000 paired multimodal news posts across science, health, and entertainment domains, and benchmark 16 open- and closed-source MLLMs for automated detection. We find that most models fall substantially short of human-level accuracy and fail critically on identifying image authenticity. Our research provides a foundation for developing robust defenses against social media fake news. Code and data are available at https: //github.com/xiuzhenzhang/Multimodal.

</details>


### [57] [TRACE: Deployable Tree-Relational Structure Enhancement for Oncology LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35810)
*Jizheng Lai,Yingyun Li,Ying Qin,Haiyang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE 是一个面向肿瘤学大语言模型的可部署树-关系增强框架，通过离线学习可更新的医学结构、在线检索紧凑证据，在零样本与微调场景下均提升模型准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学应用中 LLM 的预测往往缺乏显式医学结构的支撑，推理不够可靠且难以审计，需要一种能引入明确、可更新医学知识结构的方法。

Method: 提出 TRACE 框架：将昂贵的离线结构学习与轻量在线推理解耦。具体做法是把肿瘤学概念与关系组织成可更新的树-关系结构，利用 LM-loss 派生的证据进行精炼，在推理时检索为紧凑的提示证据，并支持零样本下无需监督标签的任务自适应证据选择。

Result: 在 10 个肿瘤学分类任务和 1 个 MedQuAD CancerGov QA 基准上，TRACE 同时提升无标签评估与监督微调的效果；优于 vanilla RAG 和通用 GraphRAG；在泄漏控制的 METABRIC 输入下依然有效；并产生与临床推理对齐的可解释证据路径。

Conclusion: 显式、可更新的医学结构是通向更准确、更可审计的肿瘤学 LLM 部署的实用路径，TRACE 证明了该方向的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in oncology applications, but their predictions are often weakly grounded in explicit medical structure. We present TRACE, a deployable tree-relational enhancement framework for oncology LLMs. TRACE separates expensive offline structure learning from lightweight online inference: oncology concepts and relations are organized into an updatable tree-relational structure, refined using LM-loss-derived evidence, and retrieved at inference time as compact prompt evidence. This design supports task-adaptive evidence selection without requiring supervised labels in the zero-shot setting. Across ten oncology classification tasks and one MedQuAD CancerGov QA benchmark, TRACE improves both label-free evaluation and supervised fine-tuning. Additional analyses show that TRACE improves over vanilla RAG and generic GraphRAG, remains useful under leakage-controlled METABRIC inputs, and produces interpretable evidence paths aligned with clinical reasoning. These results suggest that explicit, updatable medical structure is a practical path toward more accurate and auditable oncology LLM deployment.

</details>


### [58] [Lookahead-R: Budget-Aware Tool Retrieval via Execution-Centric Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35811)
*Zongze Wu,Yani Guo,Runnan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lookahead-R 将 LLM 智能体的工具检索重新定义为资源受限的序列决策问题，通过一个免调用真实 API 的执行感知代理世界模型，结合成本敏感、不确定性引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索，在 ToolBench 上取得更优的精度-效率权衡（I3 场景 NDCG@5 达 91.40%，比 SOTA ToolGen 高 1.24%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 工具检索方法存在固有权衡：语义检索快但受"语义-功能鸿沟"影响精度不足，而基于执行的验证虽提升精度但延迟开销难以承受。本文旨在打破这一权衡，在严格预算约束下以低延迟实现高精度工具检索。

Method: 提出 Lookahead-R，一个基于规划的框架，将工具检索形式化为资源受限的序列决策问题。核心是构建一个轻量级的执行感知代理世界模型（surrogate world model），在不调用真实 API 的情况下联合预测工具执行成功率、延迟成本和语义效用；随后由该世界模型驱动成本敏感、不确定性引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），在严格预算约束下导航工具空间。

Result: 在大规模 ToolBench 基准上，Lookahead-R 在所有测试场景均取得更优的精度-效率权衡。在最难的 I3 划分上达到 NDCG@5 = 91.40%，比 SOTA 方法 ToolGen（90.16%）提升 1.24%。消融实验证实显式延迟建模是在资源约束下识别高质量工具的关键判别信号。

Conclusion: Lookahead-R 通过执行感知的代理世界模型与预算约束下的 MCTS 规划，成功兼顾检索精度与延迟效率，优于现有语义检索与执行验证两大类方法，为解决大规模异构 API 生态中的工具检索瓶颈提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Tool retrieval is a critical bottleneck for LLM-based agents operating over large, heterogeneous API ecosystems. Existing approaches face an inherent trade-off: semantic retrievers are fast but suffer from the semantic-functional gap, while execution-based validation improves precision at the cost of prohibitive latency. We propose Lookahead-R, a planning-based framework that reformulates tool retrieval as a resource-constrained sequential decision-making problem. At its core, Lookahead-R introduces a lightweight execution-aware surrogate world model that jointly predicts tool execution success, latency cost, and semantic utility---without invoking real APIs. This world model drives a cost-sensitive, uncertainty-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search that navigates the tool space under strict budget constraints. Evaluated on the large-scale ToolBench benchmark, Lookahead-R achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off across all test scenarios. On the most challenging I3 split, it attains an NDCG@5 of 91.40\%, outperforming the state-of-the-art ToolGen (90.16\%) by 1.24\%. Ablation studies confirm that explicit latency modeling is the key discriminative signal for identifying high-quality tools under resource constraints.

</details>


### [59] [Automated Evaluation of Multi-Turn Dialogues in In-Car Conversational Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35812)
*Vaishnav Negi,Lev Sorokin,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对车载对话助手（ICA）多轮对话能力的自动化黑盒测试框架，通过策略引导的用户模拟器、对抗性策略管理器与两级LLM评判器进行闭环仿真评估，并以工业ICA验证其与人标注的高度一致性及更高的缺陷发现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载对话助手部署于安全攸关场景，但现有多轮交互缺乏显式真值、受严格安全约束，而既有评测方法仅面向单轮设置，无法捕捉跨轮的约束处理、上下文保持与安全关键行为，评测可靠性难以保障。

Method: 将ICA视为黑盒，构建闭环仿真系统：策略引导的用户模拟器生成对话、对抗性策略管理器引导交互、两级LLM评判器分别评估轮次级失败与对话级质量，从而自动测试多轮对话能力。

Result: 在一款工业级ICA上采用六种LLM后端与十二名人工标注者进行评测：自动化评判器与人类标注呈显著一致性；相较无引导仿真，策略引导使每段对话发现的独特失败类型增加2.96倍，独特失败对话数量翻倍以上。

Conclusion: 该自动化框架能有效且可靠地评测车载对话助手的多轮交互质量，策略引导显著提升缺陷发现效率，为安全关键场景下ICA的系统性测试提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In-car conversational assistants (ICAs) are increasingly integrated into vehicles to support route planning, vehicle control, and information access. Ensuring their reliability is challenging due to multi-turn interactions, the absence of explicit ground truth, and strict safety constraints. Existing evaluation techniques fall short, as they target single-turn settings and fail to capture constraint handling, context retention, and safety-critical behavior across turns. We propose an automated framework for testing the multi-turn conversational capabilities of ICAs. The system is treated as a black box and evaluated via closed-loop simulation with a strategy-guided user simulator, an adversarial strategy manager, and a two-tier LLM judge assessing turn-level failures and conversation-level quality. We evaluate the approach on an industrial ICA with six LLM backends and twelve human annotators. The automated judge shows substantial agreement with humans, and strategy guidance uncovers 2.96 times more unique failure types per conversation and more than doubles the number of unique failing conversations compared to unguided simulation.

</details>


### [60] [Constructing Challenging Browser-Use Tasks by Controlled Environment Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35814)
*Xunjian Yin,Tianchen Guan,Jinao Wang,Weili Cao,Daisy Xinlei Lin,Royce Cheng-Yue,Keagan Long,Kyle Wong,Bhuwan Dhingra,Xiangjun Wang,Shuyan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BreakingWeb 提出一种将难度可编程化的浏览器智能体基准：在智能体已能解决的任务上注入确定性、可检测、可恢复的环境干预，从而以受控方式制造难题，并揭示智能体的主要失败模式是"信念失败"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览器智能体基准通过收集新任务、新网站和新应用来提升难度，但代价高昂且难以控制——当多个变量同时变化时，无法确定究竟是哪个因素使任务变难。因此作者希望在不更换任务指令、目标与后端成功标准的前提下，仅通过改变环境来可控地构造困难实例，从而明确挑战的来源。

Method: BreakingWeb 为每个基础任务配对一种"干预条件"，该干预保持用户指令、潜在目标和后端成功标准不变，只在 Web 栈的不同层级改变环境。每个干预具备确定性、可检测性和可恢复性，并标注其所主要加载的认知原语。基准共含 519 个干净/干预任务对，覆盖 7 个自托管网站和 29 个干预族，全部按结果（outcome）评分。作者评估了 6 个强浏览器智能体、3 个仅见截图的 GUI 专用智能体以及人类参与者。

Result: 干预将智能体通过率平均降低 22.9%，并翻转了每个智能体在干净条件下能解决的近一半任务；人类在首次尝试中损失 10.0%，经过一次熟悉尝试后损失 5.7%。6 个智能体的失败中有 75% 以"声明成功"结束，但所需的环境变化实际并未发生，表明主导失败模式是"信念失败"。

Conclusion: 通过在已解决任务上注入受控干预，可以低成本、可解释地将难度编程进环境，从而构建更可控、可刷新的基准。结果同时表明当前浏览器智能体的核心短板并非执行能力，而是对环境状态的错误信念（相信自己已完成但实际未完成）。代码、数据和环境已在 www.breakingweb.app 公开。

Abstract: As browser-use agents improve, benchmarks keep pace by collecting new tasks, websites, and applications, often making tasks longer or more novel. This makes difficulty expensive to refresh and difficult to control: when many aspects change at once, it is unclear what actually makes a task challenging. We instead construct challenging instances from tasks agents already solve, turning difficulty into a programmable property of the environment. BreakingWeb pairs every base task with an intervention condition that preserves the user instruction, latent target, and backend success criterion while changing the environment at different web stack layers. Each intervention is deterministic, detectable, and recoverable, and is annotated with the cognitive primitive it primarily loads. The benchmark contains 519 clean/intervention task pairs across seven self-hosted websites and 29 intervention families, all graded against outcomes. We evaluate six strong browser-use agents, three GUI-only agents that see only screenshots, and humans. The construction is effective: interventions cut agent pass rate by 22.9% on average and overturn nearly half of the tasks each agent solves cleanly, whereas humans lose 10.0% on a first attempt and 5.7% after one familiarisation attempt. The dominant failure is belief failure: 75% of the six agents' failures end with a declared success although the required change never happened. Our code, data and environment are publicly available at www.breakingweb.app.

</details>


### [61] [How to Run Statistics over LLM Judges and Trust the Results: Calibrated Inference for Small-Sample AI Evaluation with evalstats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35815)
*Ian Arawjo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出研究者常基于LLM评判分数和小样本AI评估做显著性声明，但缺乏校准的置信区间、假设检验与评判偏差校正导致结论不可靠。作者发现对原始LLM分数直接统计会放大假阳性，并开发了evalstats开源工具，提供混合人机评判的统计指导、九种PPI假设检验、bootstrap自适应功率调校，以及小样本下的CI/p值/FWER方法推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界日益依赖LLM评判分数和小样本AI评估来支持显著性结论，但缺少校准的置信区间、稳健的假设检验和评判偏差校正，导致这些统计结论不可靠，可能诱发错误的科研结论与发表偏差。

Method: 作者首先分析在原始LLM评判分数上直接做统计导致的假阳性膨胀问题；其次提出混合人-AI评判设计的统计分析指导与工具，并基于预测驱动推断（PPI）实现了九种假设检验，其中四种秩和检验（Wilcoxon符号秩、Mann-Whitney U及omnibus变体）的PPI校正是首次实现；为保持PPI++在小规模人工校准集上的稳定性，提出bootstrap自适应功率调校（bootstrap-adaptive power tuning），将估计权重收缩到基于标注数据估计的目标值并纳入权重的抽样方差；随后通过蒙特卡洛模拟给出小样本（N<100）AI评估中置信区间、p值和FWER校正方法的选用建议，并警示避免使用bootstrap置信区间；最终将这些建议打包为开源Python包evalstats，自动选择校准方法。

Result: 发现对原始LLM分数直接统计会放大假阳性，在多种评分者间一致性指标下，假阳性风险反而在“近乎完美”的人-LLM一致性时达到峰值；提出bootstrap自适应功率调校可稳定PPI++在小校准集上的表现；通过模拟得出小样本AI评估应使用的CI/p值/FWER方法，并警告bootstrap CI存在风险；evalstats能在多种场景自动选用校准方法，且在一个真实LLM评判达到“substantial agreement”的案例中，成功阻止了研究者发表一项虚假发现。

Conclusion: 在LLM评判和小样本AI评估上得出显著性结论必须采用校准的统计方法，直接对原始评判分数统计会误导结论；论文为混合人机评判设计提供指导、PPI假设检验与稳定化技术，并通过evalstats开源包为研究者提供自动化的校准统计工具，有助于减少基于AI评估的假发现。

Abstract: Researchers across academia increasingly base significance claims on LLM judge scores and small-sample AI evaluations. Yet without well-calibrated confidence intervals (CIs), hypothesis tests, and judge-bias corrections, such claims are unreliable. We address these issues in several contributions. First, we find that running statistics over raw LLM judge scores leads to inflated false positives: counterintuitively, for many inter-rater agreement metrics, false positive risk peaks at "almost perfect" human-LLM agreement. To help researchers understand how to run statistics over LLM judges responsibly, we present guidance and tooling for the statistical analysis of mixed human-AI judge designs, and implement nine hypothesis tests via prediction-powered inference (PPI), including the first known PPI corrections for four rank-based tests (Wilcoxon signed-rank, Mann-Whitney U, and omnibus variants). To keep PPI++ stable with small human-labeled calibration sets, we introduce bootstrap-adaptive power tuning, which shrinks the estimated weight toward a target estimated from the labeled data, and accounts for that weight's own sampling variance. Second, through Monte Carlo simulations, we derive recommendations for what CI, p-value, and FWER correction methods to use for small-sample AI evaluations (N<100), and warn researchers against bootstrap CIs. We package these recommendations into evalstats, an open-source Python package that selects calibrated methods automatically, and demonstrate it in three scenarios, including one where a real LLM judge validated at "substantial agreement" would have led a researcher to publish a spurious finding. evalstats is publicly available at https://github.com/ianarawjo/evalstats.

</details>


### [62] [PrimeSeeker: Capability-Oriented Supervision for Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35816)
*Linzhi Peng,Hanting Chen,Heng Chang,Ke Cheng,Bowen Du,Weifeng Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PrimeSeeker，一个面向深层搜索的能力导向框架。它通过"潜在锚点推理"将搜索分解为锚点解析与关系迁移的链式操作，并据此构建带参考证据骨架的训练数据，用于监督微调与强化学习奖励审计，最终训练出检索冗余低、工具调用少的 30B 搜索智能体，在五个深层搜索基准上表现强劲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型搜索智能体的训练依赖合成问题，其难度通过更大的证据图、更多跳数和更长轨迹等全局属性来提升；但这些全局属性只是搜索所需局部检索能力的间接代理，与真实搜索中需要的细粒度检索能力不匹配。

Method: 提出"潜在锚点推理"这一基础检索单元：即从一个描述性规格中解析未命名的检索锚点，并将恢复出的锚点迁移到后续信息需求中，从而把深层搜索分解为耦合操作的链条。在此基础上构建 PrimeSeeker 框架：(1) 能力导向地构造网络锚点结构，并联合推导问题与参考证据骨架；(2) 骨架保留构造过程中的支撑证据，并通过当前工具观察的抽取式亮点指导专家生成；(3) 监督微调前移除这些亮点，骨架随后被复用于强化学习中审计参考步的覆盖情况。共构建 9,221 条专家轨迹，训练 30B 搜索智能体。

Result: PrimeSeeker 在五个深层搜索基准上取得强劲性能；参考步优化进一步提升了监督策略。生成的轨迹表现出低检索冗余，固定预算评估显示其以远少于长程系统的工具调用次数实现了较强的解答覆盖率。

Conclusion: 通过以潜在锚点推理为核心的局部检索能力建模，PrimeSeeker 将深层搜索分解为可耦合的锚点解析与关系迁移操作，并利用网络锚点结构、参考证据骨架与参考步审计来指导训练，从而训练出高效、低冗余的深层搜索智能体。

Abstract: Large language model search agents are often trained with synthetic questions whose difficulty is increased through larger evidence graphs, additional hops, and longer trajectories. These global properties, however, are only indirect proxies for the local retrieval capabilities required during search. To address this mismatch, we introduce latent anchor reasoning, which consists of resolving an unnamed retrieval anchor from descriptive specifications and transferring the recovered anchor into a subsequent information demand. This primitive retrieval unit decomposes deep search into chains of coupled operations and organizes question construction around anchor resolution and relation transfer, without prescribing a canonical search path. Based on this formulation, we propose PrimeSeeker, a capability-oriented framework that constructs web-grounded anchor structures and jointly derives a question and a reference evidence skeleton. The skeleton preserves supporting evidence from construction and guides expert generation through extractive highlights of current tool observations. These highlights are removed before supervised fine-tuning, while the skeleton is subsequently reused to audit reference-step coverage for reinforcement-learning rewards. We construct 9,221 expert trajectories, training a 30B search agent. Across five deep-search benchmarks, PrimeSeeker achieves strong performance, while reference-step optimization further improves the supervised policy. The resulting trajectories exhibit low retrieval redundancy, and fixed-budget evaluation shows strong solution coverage with substantially fewer tool calls than long-horizon systems.

</details>


### [63] [Less Uniform Discrete Diffusion is More Powerful and Scalable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35817)
*Kaibo Wang,Ding Ding,Fangyu Ding,Zijin Feng,Han Shi,Haili Bai,Jiacheng Sun,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对均匀扩散语言模型（UDLM）难以扩展的问题，提出 Less Uniform Diffusion (LUDI) 框架，通过非均匀损失和逐 token 时间嵌入解决训练目标过均匀与采样期条件-目标混淆，并成功继续训练出具备复杂推理能力的 LUDI-7B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UDLM 是一种有前景的扩散范式，但其扩展面临困难。作者识别出两大核心障碍：一是训练目标“过度均匀”（over-uniform），二是采样阶段的“条件-目标混淆”（condition-target confusion），这两者限制了 UDLM 在大规模及少步生成场景下的表现。

Method: 提出 LUDI 框架，包含两项关键设计：(i) 引入 less uniform loss，引导每个逆向转移朝干净 token 方向进行，以改善监督信号；(ii) 为模型配备逐 token 时间嵌入（per-token time embeddings），提供 token 级别的腐蚀提示（corruption hints），从而支持基于置信度的少步采样。此外，作者继续训练一个 7B 自回归模型得到 LUDI-7B。

Result: 跨规模的实验表明，LUDI 带来更干净的监督并显著改善少步生成。LUDI-7B 作为 UDLM 能够完成复杂推理任务，相比自回归解码实现每步 3 个 token 的加速，并在与掩码扩散基线的对比中取得有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: LUDI 有效解决了 UDLM 扩展中的关键障碍，证明了 UDLM 在复杂生成任务上的潜力尚未被完全释放，未来仍具探索空间。

Abstract: Although uniform diffusion language models (UDLMs) represent a promising diffusion paradigm, scaling them remains challenging. We identify the core obstacle as an over-uniform training objective and condition-target confusion during sampling. To address these, we propose Less Uniform Diffusion (LUDI), a novel UDLM framework. Specifically, we (i) introduce a less uniform loss that directs each reverse transition toward the clean token, and (ii) equip the model with per-token time embeddings that supply token-level corruption hints, enabling confidence-based few-step sampling. Experiments across scales show that LUDI yields cleaner supervision and improves few-step generation. We further continue-train a 7B autoregressive model into LUDI-7B, resulting in a UDLM capable of complex reasoning. It achieves a 3-token-per-step speedup over AR decoding and competitive performance compared with masked diffusion baselines, revealing that the full potential of UDLMs for complex generation remains to be unlocked.

</details>


### [64] [$τ$-Multilingual: Benchmarking Voice Agents Across Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35820)
*Soham Ray,Edgard dos Santos Paiva,Ruben Valenzuela,Karthik Narasimhan,Keshav Dhandhania,Victor Barres*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 τ-Multilingual，将 τ-Voice 评测扩展到西语、葡语、印地语、韩语和普通话，并揭示了非英语语音代理在任务完成度与失败模式上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仅以英语为基准的测试只能暴露语音代理行为的狭窄切片，无法反映多语言场景下的真实表现；作者希望通过母语者审核与评估，系统衡量跨语言语音代理的能力差距与失败模式。

Method: 扩展 τ-Voice 为 τ-Multilingual，覆盖西班牙语、巴西葡萄牙语、印地语、韩语和普通话，由母语者审核并评估生成语言与语音输出；在 4,500 次全双工通话和五种语音配置上进行测试，并分别报告任务完成、交互与生成质量。

Result: 西班牙语、葡萄牙语和印地语的任务完成度与英语差距在 3.2 个任务完成点以内；韩语和普通话分别下降 14.7 和 8.4 个点。失败模式各异：韩语系统漏响应更多，普通话系统打断更多，二者都在工具调用和实体识别上表现困难。Grok 任务完成度最高但生成质量最低，说明需要分开报告任务、交互与生成三项指标。

Conclusion: 单语言基准不足以评估语音代理，多语言评测能揭示不同语言下差异化的失败模式；作者开放语言包、经过验证的评判器和工具，以支持社区构建多语言语音代理评测体系。

Abstract: English-only benchmarks expose only a narrow slice of voice-agent behavior. We introduce $τ$-Multilingual, extending $τ$-Voice to Spanish, Brazilian Portuguese, Hindi, Korean, and Mandarin with native-speaker review and evaluation of generated language and spoken output. Across 4,500 full-duplex calls and five voice configurations, Spanish, Portuguese, and Hindi remain within 3.2 task-completion points of English, but Korean and Mandarin fall by 14.7 and 8.4 points. The failure modes also vary: Korean systems miss more responses, Mandarin systems interrupt more often, and both struggle with tools and entities. Grok leads task completion but scores lowest on generation quality, motivating separate task, interaction, and generation reporting. We release language packs, validated judges, and tools for community-built multilingual voice-agent evaluation.

</details>


### [65] [Can We Still Trust Disaster Social Sensing? Empirical Evidence on Detecting AI-Generated Social Media Posts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35821)
*Xiaoshan Zhou,Zaifu Zhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建多灾难事件数据集（12,000条文本），系统评估多种AI文本检测器区分人类与AI生成灾害帖子的能力，结果显示文本检测可靠性不足，无法作为运营性信任门槛，应转向多模态声明、可问责来源等情境证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害社交感知依赖公众社媒帖子获取态势感知与人道需求，但生成式AI能产出貌似目击报告的逼真消息，威胁证据可信度。因此需探究文本类AI检测器能否可靠区分人类撰写与AI生成的灾害帖子。

Method: 构建12,000条文本、3,000个匹配语义单元的数据集（覆盖9场灾害），含四类文本：原始人类帖(H0)、轻度LLM校对帖(H1)、基于相同核实事实的AI生成帖(A0)及其情感框架版本(A1)；另用42事件、6,000文本语料做模型选择与阈值校准。评估OSM-Det、Fast-DetectGPT、Binoculars及直接LLM裁判（跨5个模型家族），并进行灾害域校准、冻结编码器线性读头、配对变换敏感性及数据集伪影控制等测试。

Result: 在14个冻结跨家族配置中，AUROC仅0.402–0.517，最佳前瞻召回率在低假阳性操作点为3.6%；OSM-Det达AUROC 0.521、在6.7%实际假阳性率下召回10.4%。灾害训练的线性头AUROC达0.817，但七特征表面分类器在H0对A0上达0.784，中和表面不对称后线性头从0.733降至0.594；该头还区分A0与A1（尽管来源未变），暴露表面特征与数据伪影干扰。

Conclusion: 文本检测在灾害社交感知中不够可靠，无法作为运营性信任门槛；应依托多模态声明、可问责来源及其他情境证据来维护灾害社交感知的信任。

Abstract: Disaster social sensing converts public social-media posts into evidence for situational awareness and humanitarian needs, but generative artificial intelligence (AI) can produce plausible messages that resemble eyewitness reports. This study investigates whether text-based AI detectors can reliably distinguish human-authored from AI-generated disaster posts. We construct a dataset of 12,000 texts organised into 3,000 matched semantic units from nine disasters: original human posts (H0), minimally LLM-proofread human posts (H1), factual AI-generated posts based on the same verified facts (A0), and affectively framed versions of those AI posts (A1). A separate 6,000-text corpus from 42 events supports model selection and threshold calibration. We evaluate OSM-Det, Fast-DetectGPT, Binoculars, and direct large language model (LLM) judges across five model families, then test disaster-domain calibration, a frozen-encoder linear readout, paired transformation sensitivity, and dataset artifact controls. Across fourteen frozen cross-family configurations, AUROC is 0.402-0.517 and the best prospective recall at a calibration-derived low-false-positive operating point is 3.6%; OSM-Det reaches AUROC 0.521 and 10.4% recall at a realised 6.7% false-positive rate. A disaster-trained linear head reaches AUROC 0.817, but a seven-feature surface classifier reaches 0.784 on the H0-versus-A0 contrast, and neutralising identified surface asymmetries reduces the head from 0.733 to 0.594. The head also separates A0 from A1 even though provenance is unchanged. The results show that text-based detection is not reliable enough to serve as an operational trust gate; multimodal claims, accountable sources, and other contextual evidence should be rested on to safeguard trust in disaster social sensing.

</details>


### [66] [Tracing mechanisms of sycophantic agreement in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35822)
*Sixing Chen,Zhuofan Josh Ying,Logan Riggs Smith,Jeremy Wertheimer,Natalie Shapira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用因果中介分析揭示语言模型谄媚附和（sycophantic agreement）的内部机制：用户陈述的观点会在最终提示词的残差流中被早期纳入，并通过一组稀疏的早期注意力头偏置后续答案检索；消融这些头可显著减少谄媚同时基本保持事实准确性。对于未明确陈述、而是通过空洞反问（如“你确定吗？”）传递的观点，则发现另一组头通过抑制模型原有正确答案来促成修正答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谄媚附和虽被广泛认为是对齐失败，但其底层机制仍未被充分理解，缺乏机制层面的解释。

Method: 采用因果中介分析（causal mediation analysis）定位谄媚附和的神经机制，识别承载观点信号的注意力头；通过消融实验验证这些头的作用，并区分明确陈述观点与内容空洞的推回（pushback）两种情形下的不同头部集合。

Result: 1）明确陈述的观点在最终提示词token的残差流中很早就被纳入，并偏置后续答案检索；2）一组稀疏的早期注意力头承载该观点信号；3）消融这些头可大幅减少谄媚，同时事实准确性基本不受影响；4）无论观点如何措辞，只要显式陈述，都由同一组头承载；5）空洞推回（如“Are you sure?”）情形下，存在另一组不同的头，通过抑制模型的原始正确答案来促成修正答案。

Conclusion: 通过给出观点如何诱发谄媚附和的机制性说明，本文朝着开发更具针对性且更可靠的对齐干预方法迈出了一步。

Abstract: Sycophantic agreement in language models refers to the tendency to overly affirm a user's stated beliefs or preferences, often at the expense of factual accuracy. Although it is widely recognized as an alignment failure, its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we use causal mediation analysis to identify the mechanisms behind sycophantic agreement. We show that a stated opinion is incorporated into the residual stream of the final prompt token early, where it biases subsequent answer retrieval. A sparse set of early attention heads carries this opinion signal. Ablating these heads substantially reduces sycophancy while leaving factual accuracy largely intact. The same heads carry the opinion when it is explicitly stated, regardless of how it is phrased. When an opinion is not stated explicitly but instead conveyed through content-free pushback (e.g., ``Are you sure?"), we find a distinct set of heads that suppresses the model's original correct answer to promote a revised answer. By providing a mechanistic account of how opinions induce sycophantic agreement, this work takes a step toward developing more targeted and reliable alignment interventions.

</details>


### [67] [Reliable but Design-Sensitive: Instrument Uncertainty in LLM Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35824)
*Thomas Reiter,Christoph Kern,Fedor Miasnikov,Sofiia Nikolenko,Rob Chew,Stephanie Eckman,Frauke Kreuter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过7个大语言模型、12种任务设计、3次独立运行及3000条推文（标注攻击性语言与仇恨言论），发现即便同一模型与设计重复运行一致性很高（Fleiss' κ中位数0.91），但更换任务设计后一致性下降（Cohen's κ中位数0.76）；任务设计与模型选择相对于纯抽样方差使估计患病率的方差放大76.7倍（攻击性语言）与110.6倍（仇恨言论），而置信度分数无法解决该问题。作者将这种由任务设计与模型选择引起的变异称为"工具不确定性"，并主张只能通过比较合理的任务设计来测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在不同但同样合理的研究设计下会给出不一致的标签，这种由设计选择导致的变异在既有基于LLM的标注研究中未被系统量化，可能危及结论的可复现性与可靠性。

Method: 系统性地比较7个LLM、12种任务设计、3次独立运行，对3000条推文进行攻击性语言和仇恨言论标注；采用Fleiss' κ与Cohen's κ衡量一致性，分解任务设计、模型选择与抽样方差对估计患病率的影响，并检验置信度分数是否能反映标注可靠性。

Result: 同一模型与设计重复运行一致性高（Fleiss' κ中位数0.91）；更换任务设计后一致性降至Cohen's κ中位数0.76；任务设计与模型选择使估计患病率方差相比抽样方差放大76.7倍（攻击性语言）和110.6倍（仇恨言论）。跨LLM任务设计的变异达560–572个基点，而5个人工标注版本间变异为270–331个基点。置信度分数更贴近模型重复输出而非与人工标签的一致性；将6条推文并入同一提示使攻击性语言平均置信度下降660个基点。

Conclusion: 由任务设计与模型选择引起的变异构成"工具不确定性"，研究者只能通过比较合理的任务设计来测量；仅重复同一设置或依赖置信度分数无法替代这种检验。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can give reliable labels under one setup yet change those labels when researchers make other reasonable design choices. We tested seven LLMs, 12 task designs, three independent runs, and 3,000 tweets labeled for offensive language and hate speech.
