Repeating the same model and task design produced high agreement (median Fleiss' $κ= 0.91$). Agreement fell when we changed the task design for the same tweets (median Cohen's $κ= 0.76$). Task design and model choice increased the variance of estimated prevalence by factors of 76.7 for offensive language and 110.6 for hate speech compared with sampling variance alone. Variation across LLM task designs reached 560-572 basis points, compared with 270-331 basis points across five human instrument versions. Confidence scores did not solve this problem. They tracked repeated model outputs more closely than agreement with human labels, and grouping six tweets in one prompt lowered mean offensive-language confidence by 660 basis points. We call the variation caused by task design and model choice instrument uncertainty. Researchers can measure it only by comparing reasonable task designs. Repeating one setup or relying on confidence scores cannot replace that test.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond the Context Window: An Adaptive Entropy-Based Routing Framework for Hybrid Retrieval and Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35831)
*Isaac Olufadewa,Miracle Adesina,Ezekiel Oladejo,Owen Adeniyi,Fadare Fadekemi,Olamide Oso,Uthman Babatunde,Matthew Olawoyin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EDAR（Entropy-Driven Adaptive Router），一种在推理时依据预测熵在 RAG 与长上下文处理之间动态路由的框架，在保住大部分准确率的同时大幅降低 token 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 支持超百万 token 的上下文窗口，引发"RAG 是否仍有必要"的争论。纯长上下文处理成本高且对长输入中段信息关注不足；纯 RAG 快但受检索质量限制，在检索块部分相关或相互矛盾时易出错。因此需要一个既能控制成本又能保证质量的混合路由方案。

Method: EDAR 在推理时决定是使用检索块回答查询，还是升级为完整长上下文处理。决策依据是 RAG 响应生成的前几个 token 的 token 级概率分布的预测熵；熵阈值在留出验证集上通过扫描成本与准确率来确定。框架不依赖特定检索器或 LC 主干，也不需解码之外额外的监督。

Result: 在 2000 条生成的留出集上，预测熵与幻觉率强相关（Pearson r=0.85，95% CI [0.83,0.87]）。在 LongBench v2 与 Infinity-Bench 上，EDAR 保留纯长上下文基线 97.4% 的准确率，同时将总 token 支出降低 70.7%，仅升级 18.2% 的查询；与纯长上下文系统的准确率差距在标准样本量下与零无统计可区分差异。

Conclusion: 预测熵是介于 RAG 与长上下文推理之间进行路由的有用模型内部信号；基于阈值的混合系统能以极低成本恢复长上下文模型的大部分准确率。该框架通用、轻量且无需额外监督。

Abstract: Modern large language models now support context windows of more than one million tokens, which has raised the question of whether retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is still necessary. Pure long-context (LC) processing is expensive and is known to under-attend to information placed in the middle of long inputs, while pure RAG is fast but bounded by retrieval quality and prone to errors when retrieved chunks are partially relevant or contradictory. We propose the Entropy-Driven Adaptive Router (EDAR), a framework that decides at inference time whether to answer a query from retrieved chunks or to escalate it to full long-context processing. The decision uses the predictive entropy of the token-level probability distribution computed over the first few generated tokens of the RAG response. The entropy threshold is selected on a held-out validation set by sweeping cost against accuracy. Experiments compare EDAR against pure-RAG and pure-LC baselines on LongBench v2 and Infinity-Bench. Predictive entropy correlates strongly with hallucination rate on a held-out set of 2,000 generations (Pearson r = 0.85, 95% CI [0.83, 0.87]). On the long-context benchmarks, EDAR retains 97.4% of the accuracy of the pure long-context baseline while reducing total token expenditure by 70.7%, escalating only 18.2% of incoming queries. The accuracy gap between EDAR and the pure long-context system is not statistically distinguishable from zero at standard sample sizes. Predictive entropy is a useful model-internal signal for routing between RAG and long-context inference, and a threshold-based hybrid system can recover most of the accuracy of long-context models at a small fraction of the cost. The framework does not depend on a specific retriever or LC backbone, and it does not require additional supervision beyond what is normally produced during decoding.

</details>


### [69] [When Should LLMs Trust Their Own Revisions? A Risk-Aware Study of Intrinsic Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35832)
*Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文研究大语言模型内在自我纠错（无外部证据的自我修改答案）的利弊权衡，追踪初始答案与修订答案之间的正确性转移，发现自我纠错既能修复错误也会推翻正确答案，主张应将其视为一种"修订策略"而非总是有益的二次推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点倾向于把内在自我纠错视为普遍有益的获取第二次推理机会的手段，但作者发现第二次推理既可能修复错误，也可能推翻原本正确的答案。整体准确率这一聚合指标会掩盖这两种截然不同的修订行为，因此需要更精细地剖析"修复"与"损害"之间的权衡。

Method: 在29个开放权重大语言模型上，于BoolQ、GSM8K和Corr2Cause三个基准任务中，追踪初始答案与修订答案之间的正确性转移。通过受控的BoolQ研究考察修订提示如何改变修复与损害之间的平衡。随后比较三种运行时策略：保留初始答案、总是接受修订、以及仅利用初始响应后可获得的信号有选择地（门控式）调用修订。

Result: 聚合准确率掩盖了差异显著的修订行为：例如Llama-3.1-8B在GSM8K上提升25.5个百分点，但同时将19.1%原本正确的答案改错。受控研究显示修订提示会改变修复与损害之间的平衡。策略比较揭示出某些场景下学习式门控更有效，而其他场景下更简单的无条件策略反而表现更好。

Conclusion: 应将内在自我纠错视为一种"修订策略"而非普遍有益的二次推理，并通过其修复的错误与引入的新的错误两方面进行联合评估。

Abstract: Intrinsic self-correction asks a language model to revise its own answer without receiving new external evidence. A second pass can recover mistakes, but it can also overturn answers that were already correct. We study this trade-off across 29 open-weight LLMs on BoolQ, GSM8K, and Corr2Cause by tracking correctness transitions between initial and revised answers. Aggregate accuracy can conceal substantially different revision behavior: for example, Llama-3.1-8B improves by 25.5 percentage points on GSM8K, while refinement changes 19.1% of initially correct answers into wrong ones. A controlled BoolQ study further shows that refinement prompts shift the balance between recovery and harm. We then compare three runtime choices: keeping the initial answer, always accepting the revision, and selectively invoking revision using signals available after the initial response. The comparison identifies settings where learned gating is useful and others where a simpler unconditional policy performs better. These results suggest treating intrinsic self-correction as a revision policy rather than as a uniformly beneficial second pass, and evaluating it through both the corrections it recovers and the errors it introduces.

</details>


### [70] [Hyperspherical Semantic Trajectory Analysis: Mapping Technological Diffusion across Academic Preprints, Patent Signals, and Compute Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35845)
*Muhammad Sukri Bin Ramli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出一种无监督方法 HSTA（超球面语义轨迹分析），通过分析 arXiv 与 USPTO 的 3 万条非结构化文本（嵌入投影到单位超球面，结合球形 K-Means 与 UMAP），构建语义质心漂移与商业化偏移两个指标，用于实时追踪技术扩散，弥补宏观生产率指标（如 TFP）的多年度滞后。实证发现大语言模型（漂移 0.332）与人工智能系统（漂移 0.234）语义演化最快，且论文量增速本身不足以 Granger 引发算力投入激增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观生产率指标（如全要素生产率 TFP）因行政调查周期与国民核算惯例，对技术突破的记录存在多年滞后，难以实时捕捉新兴技术的扩散进程，因此需要一种能从海量非结构化科学文献与商业文本中直接、实时追踪技术扩散的定量方法。

Method: 提出 HSTA（超球面语义轨迹分析）：对来自 arXiv 预印本与 USPTO 专利的 3 万条过滤后文档，使用 Transformer 获取高维句嵌入，经 UMAP 流形降维后投影到单位超球面，通过球形 K-Means 在八个主要子主题上聚类；据此定义两个量化指标：(1) 语义质心向量漂移（衡量时间子语料间词汇迁移，识别结构性范式转变）；(2) 商业化偏移（评估科学发现与知识产权申请间的跨语料峰值密度对齐）。再结合 Epoch AI 物理硬件指标、季度主题量速度与向量自回归 F 检验，检验文本信号对算力投入的 Granger 因果关系。

Result: 涵盖大语言模型（漂移指标 0.332）与人工智能系统（漂移指标 0.234）的子主题经历最高速的语义演化；向量自回归 F 检验显示，仅凭季度论文量速度在常规显著性水平下并不 Granger 引发前沿算力投入激增，说明需将文本信号与物理资本约束相结合。

Conclusion: HSTA 提供了一种客观、实时的机制来补充传统经济统计，可直接从科学文献与专利文本中量化和追踪技术扩散，尤其能捕捉 LLM 与 AI 系统等快速演化领域，并揭示文本信号必须结合物理资本约束才能有效解释算力投入变化。

Abstract: Macroeconomic productivity metrics, such as Total Factor Productivity, register technological breakthroughs with multi-year reporting lags due to administrative survey intervals and national accounting conventions. This paper introduces Hyperspherical Semantic Trajectory Analysis (HSTA), an unsupervised quantitative methodology that tracks technology diffusion directly from unstructured scientific and commercial text streams. We analyze 30,000 filtered document records spanning academic preprints from arXiv and patent application records from the USPTO. By projecting high-dimensional Transformer sentence embeddings onto unit hyperspheres using Spherical K-Means clustering across eight primary sub-topics and UMAP manifold reductions, HSTA formalizes two quantitative metrics: (1) Semantic Centroid Vector Drift, which tracks vocabulary shifts between temporal sub-corpora to identify structural paradigm transformations; and (2) Commercialization Offset, which evaluates cross-corpus peak density alignments between scientific discovery and intellectual property filings. Linking quarterly topic volume velocity with physical hardware metrics from the Epoch AI database, Vector Autoregressive F-tests demonstrate that quarterly paper volume velocity alone does not Granger-cause frontier compute allocation surges at conventional statistical significance levels, highlighting the necessity of conditioning textual signals on physical capital constraints. Empirical results reveal that sub-topics covering Large Language Models (with a drift metric of 0.332) and Artificial Intelligence Systems (with a drift metric of 0.234) undergo the highest rate of semantic evolution, offering an objective, real-time mechanism to complement traditional economic statistics.

</details>


### [71] [The Detectability Gap: Hidden Heterogeneity in Hallucination Detection Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35860)
*Pranav Darshan,Pranav A,Sravan Karthick T,Minal Moharir,Ivan P. Yamshchikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究基于采样一致性的幻觉检测在不同语言模型与数据集上的异质性，发现幻觉可按答案一致性分为高一致（Ghost）与低一致（Flickering）两种机制，两者在可检测性上存在显著差异，且这种异质性被聚合指标所掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样一致性的幻觉检测被广泛使用，但其聚合性能可能掩盖不同错误类型在可检测性上的系统性差异，需要揭示这种异质性。

Method: 在四个语言模型和三个事实问答数据集上，按答案一致性将幻觉划分为高一致（Ghost）与低一致（Flickering）机制；先检验用于定义机制与度量差距的统计量之间的耦合强度，再冻结机制分配后用词汇与语义响应离散度验证不对称性；最后用不共享随机种子的单个扩散轨迹进行更严格的检验。

Result: 两种机制间存在 0.35 至 0.46 AUC 的检测差距；定义统计量高度耦合（|ρ|≈0.94 至 1.00）；冻结机制后不对称性在全部 12 个模型-数据集设置中保持（bootstrap 95% 区间不含零）；严格的扩散轨迹检验在全部三个 LLaDA 数据集上显著（p<0.005），三个 Dream 数据集方向一致且一个达到显著；难检测机制占比在模型间差异大（16% 至 77%），且同一提示在不同模型间机制常发生变化。

Conclusion: 聚合检测指标掩盖了语言模型失败中持续存在、依赖模型的异质性，因此应采用按机制（regime）条件化的评估方式。

Abstract: Sampling based consistency is widely used for hallucination detection, yet aggregate performance can conceal systematic differences in which errors are detectable. This work studies that heterogeneity across four language models and three factual question answering datasets. Partitioning hallucinations by answer agreement reveals high agreement (Ghost) and low agreement (Flickering) regimes with an apparent detectability gap of $0.35$ to $0.46$ AUC. Because the statistics used to define the regimes and measure this gap are strongly coupled ($|ρ|\approx0.94$ to $1.00$), the raw result is treated as a property of agreement based detection rather than independent evidence. After freezing regime assignments, lexical and semantic response dispersion preserve the asymmetry, with bootstrap $95\%$ intervals excluding zero in all $12$ model and dataset settings. A stricter test using individual diffusion trajectories and no cross seed information preserves the asymmetry across all three LLaDA datasets ($p<0.005$) and directionally across all three Dream datasets, with one reaching significance. The hard regime varies substantially in prevalence across models ($16\%$ to $77\%$), and matched prompts frequently change regimes between models. These findings show that aggregate detection metrics conceal persistent, model dependent heterogeneity in language model failures and motivate regime conditioned evaluation.

</details>


### [72] [Resolving the Missing Financial Data Crisis: A Generative AI Pipeline for SEC 10-K Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35864)
*Prisha Nair,Roee Shraga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种大语言模型（Llama-3 8B、Qwen-2.5 14B、Llama-3.3 70B）从 SEC 10-K 文件中提取财务属性的能力，发现模型规模与文档复杂度匹配时可获得高零样本准确率，并提出可扩展框架以解决量化金融数据集中的缺失数据偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEC 10-K 文件包含大量未结构化捕获的财务信息，造成影响超过70%公司和一半总市值的缺失数据问题，对规模较小的公司产生不成比例的偏差；传统方法（正则表达式、BERT）解析复杂且结构多变的 10-K 文件时表现脆弱，常因数据位于其他章节或脚注而丢失信息。

Method: 采用零样本提取方式，评估多款 LLM（Llama-3 8B、Qwen-2.5 14B、Llama-3.3 70B）在四个结构复杂度各异的财务变量上的提取质量：现金及现金等价物（表格型）、短期债务（混合型）、信贷便利（叙述型）和研发支出（混合型），并考察复杂负向提示对模型性能的影响。

Result: 较小模型 Llama-3 8B 在复杂负向提示下性能下降；模型参数规模与文档复杂度匹配时零样本准确率高。Qwen-2.5 14B 作为表格型数据专家，在现金项上取得 83.33% 的 F1 分数；Llama-3.3 70B 能有效处理密集叙述型脚注，在研发支出上取得 76.92% 的 F1 分数。

Conclusion: 该可扩展框架填补了量化金融数据集的关键信息缺口，通过更彻底地分析 SEC 10-K 文件消除缺失数据偏差，表明需根据文档结构复杂度选择适当规模的模型以提升提取准确率。

Abstract: SEC 10-K filings contain substantial financial information that is not consistently captured in structured datasets, creating a missing-data problem affecting over 70% of firms and half of total market capitalization. This can disproportionately bias quantitative analysis against smaller firms, which may be excluded due to limited available data. Traditional financial extraction methods such as Regular Expressions (Regex) and BERT, have been widely used. However, they are highly brittle when parsing complex SEC 10-K filings, which leads to data that is existent in the files being lost since these methods do not consider that a data attribute could be located in a different section or a footnote. This study evaluates several Large Language Models (LLMs), including Llama-3 8B, Qwen-2.5 14B, and Llama-3.3 70B, to figure out individual model strengths and weaknesses when extracting specific attributes from SEC 10-K text. The extraction quality was evaluated across four financial variables of varying structural complexity: Cash and Cash Equivalents (tabular), Short-Term Debt (hybrid), Credit Facilities (narrative), and Research and Development (hybrid). Results show that while smaller models like Llama-3 8B experience performance degradation under complex negative prompting, aligning parameter scale with document complexity yields high zero-shot accuracy. Qwen-2.5 14B excels as a tabular specialist with an 83.33% F1 score on Cash, whereas Llama-3.3 70B effectively navigates dense narrative footnotes, achieving a 76.92% F1 score on R&D. This scalable framework addresses critical information gaps in quantitative finance datasets and eliminates missing-data bias through a more thorough analysis of the SEC 10-K files.

</details>


### [73] [PACT: Pairwise-Anchored Calibrated Tuning for Single-Token Typed Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35865)
*Yida Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对单token类型化决策模型，本文提出 PACT 训练方法，将对比对结构转化为四种无需新标注的训练项加一个上下文温度模块，在保持准确率的同时显著降低位置偏差、序数误差和种子间方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单token类型化决策模型读取单个位置上少数单字母答案码的 logits，训练时采用普通交叉熵，忽略了训练数据中的大量结构信息。本文旨在利用数据中天然存在的对比对结构（两个语境仅在一条被编辑的事实上不同、且该事实翻转答案）及其机器验证的删除证明，来提升模型的校准性、稳健性与稳定性。

Method: PACT 把对比对结构转化为四个训练项和一个上下文温度模块：(1) 差分差分（difference-in-differences）间隔，对共享 logit 偏移不变；(2) 排列一致性项，对抗答案码位置偏差；(3) 证据必要性项，作用于经证书验证的消融语境；(4) 序数传输代价，用于评分量表字段；外加一个三参数的上下文温度。所有项均无需新增标注。

Result: 在 324 项冻结留出集上（三个种子），PACT 准确率与已发表配方相当（84.6% vs. 85.2%，每个种子的 McNemar p ≥ 0.50），同时取得所有运行中最低的位置偏差（重标号下答案翻转率 9.8% vs. 13.8%）和最低的评分量表字段序数误差（MAE 0.232 vs. 0.311）。与仅用交叉熵的同优化器同调度对照组相比，PACT 在三个种子中的两个显著更准确，种子间离散度减半，NLL 降低 26%。种子匹配消融与预注册证伪测试表明：没有任何单个训练项能提升原始准确率，其价值在于稳健性与稳定性而非表面准确率。

Conclusion: PACT 证明了在单token类型化决策中，显式利用对比对结构与证书验证信息可以在不牺牲准确率的前提下大幅改善位置偏差、序数误差、NLL 与训练稳定性；方法无需额外标注，核心收益在于鲁棒性而非准确率提升。代码、数据划分、训练适配器与全部运行记录已开源。

Abstract: Single-token typed-decision models answer a schema question by reading the logits of a few one-letter answer codes at a single position: they are fast and return a probability for every allowed answer, but they are trained with plain cross-entropy that ignores most of the structure in their training data. We study such a model whose data is curated as contrastive pairs---two contexts that differ in one edited fact that flips the answer---each carrying a machine-checked certificate that deleting the decisive sentence makes the fact unknown. We propose PACT, which turns this structure into four training terms that need no new annotation: a difference-in-differences margin over each pair that is invariant to any shared logit offset, a permutation-consistency term against answer-code position bias, an evidence-necessity term on certificate-verified ablated contexts, and an ordinal transport cost for rubric fields, plus a three-parameter contextual temperature. On a frozen 324-item holdout with three seeds, PACT matches the published recipe in accuracy ($84.6\%$ vs. $85.2\%$; McNemar $p \ge 0.50$ at every seed) while giving the lowest position bias of all runs (answer flips under relabelling $9.8\%$ vs. $13.8\%$) and the lowest ordinal error on rubric fields (MAE $0.232$ vs. $0.311$). Against a control with the same optimiser and schedule but cross-entropy only, PACT is significantly more accurate at two of three seeds, halves the seed-to-seed spread and lowers NLL by $26\%$. Seed-matched ablations and pre-specified falsification tests locate these gains precisely: no single term raises raw accuracy, and the method's value lies in robustness and stability rather than headline accuracy. Code, data splits, trained adapters, and all run records are available at https://github.com/BennyLinntu/PACT-Pairwise-Anchored-Calibrated-Tuning-for-Single-Token-Typed-Decisions.

</details>


### [74] [CruxBench: A Benchmark of Information Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35879)
*Hui Dai,Lina Piao,Nick Merrill,Nadja Flechner,Ezra Karger,Haifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CruxBench，一个通过信息价值（VOI）来评估大语言模型"提出关键子问题（crux）"能力的基准，具有抗污染、开放性和有依据三大特性，并发现现有前沿模型在该任务上仅略优于随机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 基准主要评估答案相对固定参考标签的准确性，但许多真实复杂任务的核心在于先"提出值得问的问题"——将难题分解为关键子问题（crux）。这一信息发现能力缺乏有效评估手段，因此需要一个能衡量模型所提问题对解决目标问题信息贡献的基准。

Method: 作者构建 CruxBench，用信息价值（Value of Information, VOI）来给 LLM 生成的子问题打分，即衡量一个模型提出的 crux 能在多大程度上更新人们对目标预测问题的信念。该基准具备三个关键特性：(1) 抗污染——真值由未来世界事件生成；(2) 开放性——接受无界的复杂文本提交而非单一正确答案；(3) 有依据——以真实世界信念的量化变化来衡量信息量。并在 293 个目标预测问题上评估了 8 个不同模型。

Result: 在 293 个目标预测问题上对 8 个模型的评估表明，VOI 与独立的模型能力指标高度相关（r=0.90），并能有效捕捉 crux 对回答目标问题的有用性。然而，信息发现任务对前沿 LLM 而言仍具挑战，因为它们仅勉强超过随机时机基线。

Conclusion: CruxBench 提供了一个抗污染、开放且基于真实世界信念量化的新基准，能有效评估 LLM 的信息发现能力。VOI 指标可靠且与模型能力高度相关，但当前前沿模型的分解提问能力仍然有限，信息发现仍是颇具挑战的开放问题。

Abstract: Benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) typically evaluate the accuracy of answers against fixed reference labels. But a central step in many complex real-world tasks is identifying which questions are worth asking in the first place: decomposing a difficult problem into subquestions -- which we call cruxes -- whose answers provide key steps on the path toward solving the target problem. To evaluate this capability of information discovery, we introduce CruxBench, a benchmark that grades LLM-generated questions by their Value of Information (VOI): how much a model-proposed crux updates beliefs about a target forecasting question. CruxBench enjoys a rare combination of three key properties: it is (1) contamination-resistant by construction, since ground truth is generated by future world events; (2) open-ended, admitting unbounded and complex text-based submissions rather than one correct numeric answer; and (3) grounded, with informativeness measured against quantified changes in real-world beliefs. We evaluate a diverse set of eight models on 293 target forecasting questions and find that VOI correlates highly with independent measures of model capability (r=0.90) and captures cruxes' usefulness for answering target questions. However, information discovery remains challenging even for frontier LLMs, which only narrowly outperform a random-timing baseline.

</details>


### [75] [Almost Human, Except When It Matters: VoxParity and the Decisions a Voice Should Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35922)
*Bhavik Mangla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: A voice agent can handle almost every call on the words alone and still fail the few its sector's rules were written for. Emergency-call standards, fraud guidance, radio phraseology and vulnerability rules recognise that how a caller sounds, or what else is audible, can change the right action. VoxParity tests whether agents act on it. In 183 scenarios from 14 sectors, one transcript stays fixed while the audio changes (a coaching voice, a medical monitor beeping, a mayday under a radio check, noise over a drug name, a child's voice placing a bet, a frightened whisper), and with it the correct typed tool call. A words-only null test credits a system only if hearing the call moves its actions more than it moves a pipeline that only reads the words. Only 11 of the 23 systems that can also be run on the transcript pass. Descriptively, errors run toward the words: when the audio calls for protection, all 28 systems carry out the routine request more often than they over-react on clean calls (41% against 12% pooled; the words-only pipeline, 58% against 15%). Exploratory analyses place most of the leading systems' misses on cues they heard; systems beat the null almost entirely on items that state the rule; the leading systems overrule heard resignation or confusion far more often than acute alarm; and, in the models tested, describing the voice and stating the rule each recover part of the shortfall, leaving a gap on emotion.

</details>


### [76] [Question-Specific Knowledge Graphs for Efficient Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35942)
*Ting-Chih Chen,Emile van Krieken,Shujian Yu,Filip Ilievski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 VisKG，一种基于强化学习的框架，让视觉语言模型将视觉输入翻译成针对具体问题的知识图谱表示，以最少的充分信息支持多步推理，在保持/提升性能的同时大幅降低 token 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉问答中的视觉到文本翻译依赖视觉细节的保留质量，但现有基于详细图像caption的方法会引入与推理无关的视觉细节，导致输入 token 膨胀、计算成本上升。作者希望在不引入虚假假设的前提下，仅保留支持推理所需的显式与隐式知识。

Method: 提出 VisKG 强化学习框架，让模型学习将视觉内容翻译为问题特定的知识图谱(KG)表示，过滤感知噪声、保留思维链推理所需的实体-关系结构，遵循"最小充分信息"原则。为保证 RL 后训练稳定性，采用 Group reward-Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization (GDPO)；并在监督阶段加入负例推理样本，让模型在 RL 前接触错误推理路径。以 GRPO 训练版本作对照。

Result: 在科学、数学和通用视觉理解基准上，VisKG 达到与基线相当或更好的性能，同时 token 数少于基于 caption 的表示；使用 GDPO 训练的 VisKG 相比 GRPO 版本平均精度提升 2%。

Conclusion: 知识图谱表示是支持多步推理的有前景的方法，未来可进一步研究为给定任务自适应选择最合适的表示形式。

Abstract: Recent work in visual question answering has shown that vision-language models can exhibit strong reasoning capabilities by translating visual inputs into textual representations. The effectiveness of this translation depends on how well visual details are retained; models need to surface and align both explicit and implicit knowledge sufficient to support reasoning, without introducing spurious assumptions. Existing methods that leverage detailed image captions introduce visual details unrelated to the reasoning task, inflating input token counts and increasing computational cost. To address these challenges, we propose VisKG, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework in which models learn to translate visual content into question-specific knowledge graph (KG) representations. This process filters out perceptual noise while preserving the entity-relation structure needed for chain-of-thought reasoning, following the principle of minimum sufficient information. To ensure stable RL post-training, VisKG adopts Group reward-Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization (GDPO). In addition, we strengthen the supervision stage with negative rationale samples, exposing the model to incorrect reasoning paths before RL post-training. Experimental results across science, mathematics, and general visual understanding benchmarks show that VisKG achieves performance comparable to or better than baselines, while requiring fewer tokens than caption-based representations. Moreover, training VisKG with GDPO improves accuracy by 2% over its GRPO-trained counterpart on average. These results suggest that KG representations are a promising approach for supporting multi-step reasoning and open up future work on adaptively selecting the most suitable representation for a given task.

</details>


### [77] [Causal and Interpretable Structures in LLM Compositional Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35970)
*Gurbir Arora,Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型如何在transformer各层中表示和处理token间的关系信息，发现存在从两token关系到三token关系的层间递进表示，且这种关系表示是因果相关的，限制模型使用这些表示能提升预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能解决依赖token间关系（而非仅单个token）的任务，但这类关系信息在transformer各层中究竟如何被表示和处理尚不清楚，因此需要从几何结构和因果机制两方面揭示其表示层面的机制。

Method: 作者构建了需要推断三个token之间循环关系（月份、小时、星期、音符）来预测下一token的提示集合，跨Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Mistral等多个模型家族，分析各层激活的几何组织方式，并结合因果干预实验，考察这些关系表示对下一token预测的因果作用。

Result: 发现一致的层间递进模式：中间层使用基于两个token推断关系的联合表示，后续层使用关联全部三个token的联合表示以正确完成任务；还存在几何上有结构但因果上惰性的其他token关系；更令人惊讶的是，限制模型仅使用因果相关的联合表示反而提升了下一token预测准确率。

Conclusion: 通过几何与因果分析结合，揭示了模型逐层组织并组合关系信息以形成答案的表示层面机制，并表明限制到因果相关联合表示可提升预测性能，为理解LLM的关系推理能力提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models are able to solve tasks whose answers depend on not only individual input tokens, but also on relations among them. How is such relational information represented and processed across transformer layers? We study activations from ensembles of prompts that require inferring relationships between three tokens corresponding to a cyclic concept (months, hours, weekdays, and musical notes) to correctly predict the next token. Across model families (Llama, Qwen, Gemma, and Mistral) and cyclic concepts, we find a consistent layerwise progression in how the joint dependence among the tokens is geometrically organized and causally used: intermediate layers use a joint representation based on the inferred relationship between two tokens, while later layers use a joint representation associated with all three tokens to correctly complete the task. We also find other relationships between tokens that are geometrically structured but remain causally inert in the next-token prediction. Crucially, when taken together, these geometric and causal investigations reveal the representation-level mechanism that progressively organizes and composes the relational information to form the answer. More surprisingly, restricting the models to such causally relevant joint representations improves next-token prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [78] [Mnemon: Raw Records, Fast Judgments, Slow Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36059)
*Guangren Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mnemon 是一种将记忆工作划分为快慢两个系统的记忆代理：System 1 决策模型 Jev 快速判断记录的相关性，System 2 LLM 负责规划搜索与组织回答，并以明确预算规则将判断结果浓缩为小 View，从而在低上下文开销下取得顶尖的长期记忆评测成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统大多在写入时将对话重写为事实、图谱或定型记忆，这种"一次定型"的做法限制了灵活性。作者提出记忆如同思考，应分为两个系统：大量小而独立的快判断（System 1）与少量慢的规划、检索与作答（System 2），以避免把所有记忆工作都交给慢速 LLM。

Method: Mnemon 保留原始带日期的对话记录；System 2（LLM）规划对其的搜索查询，System 1（Jev 决策模型）判断检索结果的相关性，显式预算规则将判断收敛为一个小型 View，再交给不改动的作答模型。后台流程将每条记录一次性整合为主题时间线、价值史与常设指令，以支撑跨整段对话的问题。由于写入时不预先定型记录，同一代理可读取任何返回带日期记录的记忆存储。

Result: 以 gpt-4.1-mini 作答时，Mnemon 在 LoCoMo 得 91.7%（14 个系统的公开复评中最高）、LongMemEval-S 得 83.8%，每题上下文不到 4k token，并在 LoCoMo 上拥有最低有效成本指数。改用推理模型作答后，LoCoMo 达 92.2%、LongMemEval-S 达 94.4%（后者与最佳已发表结果持平）。在 BEAM 上，历史从 100K 增至 10M token，每题成本仅增长 1.11 倍。Jev 在相同记录上对黄金证据的区分度优于两个 LLM，且速度快 3-11 倍。

Conclusion: 将记忆划分为快速的 System 1 判断与慢速的 System 2 规划，并用预算规则把检索结果汇总为小 View，是一种高效且可扩展的长期记忆方案。该架构不依赖写入时定型，因而具有跨存储的通用性，在准确率、上下文开销和成本扩展性上均表现优异。

Abstract: Long-term memory lets an LLM assistant use a history it can no longer reread, and most memory systems build it by rewriting conversations into facts, graphs or typed memories at write time. We argue that the work of memory divides, as thinking does, into two systems. Most of it is fast System 1 work: many small, independent yes/no judgments about records, such as whether a record is needed or no longer current, which a decision model makes by the dozen in a third of a second. Only a little is slow System 2 work: writing a few search queries, naming what the reply needs and composing the answer, which an LLM does well but slowly. We present Mnemon, a memory agent built on this division. It keeps conversations as raw, dated records; an LLM (System 2) plans searches over them, a decision model, Jev (System 1), judges what the searches return, and rules with explicit budgets turn the judgments into a small View for an unchanged answering model. A background pass consolidates each record once into topic timelines, value histories and standing instructions linked to the records, so that questions about a whole conversation reach evidence their own searches miss. Because nothing is decided about a record when it is written, the same agent can read any store that returns dated records.
  With gpt-4.1-mini answering, as in a public re-evaluation of 14 systems, Mnemon scores 91.7% on LoCoMo, the highest among them, and 83.8% on LongMemEval-S, from under 4k tokens of context per question, with the lowest effective cost index on LoCoMo. With a reasoning model answering, it reaches 92.2% on LoCoMo and 94.4% on LongMemEval-S, the latter on par with the best published results. From 100K to 10M tokens of history on BEAM, its cost per question grows by a factor of 1.11. On the same records, Jev separates gold evidence better than two LLMs and is 3-11 times faster.

</details>


### [79] [PADMÉ: Preference Alignment Data Synthesis for Meta-Evaluation of LM Agent Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36086)
*Cheng Chang,Yining Mao,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PADMÉ，一种将元评估重构为偏好判断问题的数据合成方法，仅用小语言模型、无需人工参与即可生成可靠的准则化元评估数据，并在智能体场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用语言模型（LM）作为评估器来评价其他模型在多准则的智能体行为上是否可靠，取决于其决策与人类判断是否一致，即"元评估"问题。直接解决该问题存在困难：收集人类数据昂贵、绝对评分难以对齐、用 LM 元评估器又会递归引入可信度问题。

Method: 作者将元评估重新表述为偏好判断问题：不再比较人类与 LM 评估器对同一轨迹的评分，而是判断二者隐含的偏好是否一致。基于此提出 PADMÉ 数据合成方法，仅使用小型语言模型，评估过程中无需人工参与，且在低算力预算下运行，生成准则化的智能体元评估数据。

Result: 作者构建了 PADMÉ 原型，在四个智能体领域和三个评估准则上合成了 1,000 条样本。在 150 条子集上的人工验证显示，PADMÉ 相较朴素基线将与人类判断的一致率从 73% 提升到 85%。对该数据集上 25 个常见模型的元评估表明，评估性能与评分粒度、宽松度和模型规模等因素之间存在相关性。

Conclusion: PADMÉ 提供了一种低成本、免人工参与的可靠元评估数据合成方案，有效提升与人类判断的一致性，并能揭示评估器性能与评分粒度、宽松度及模型规模等关键因素之间的关联。

Abstract: Language models are frequently employed to evaluate other language models. An LM evaluator scoring agentic behaviors across multiple criteria is valuable, provided that its decisions align with human judgment. We call the problem of evaluating this alignment Meta-Evaluation. Tackling it directly is difficult: collecting human data is expensive, absolute scoring is hard to align, and using an LM meta-evaluator recurses the question of trustworthiness. We adopt a reformulation of meta-evaluation as a preference judgment problem: rather than comparing human and LM evaluator scores of a trajectory, we ask whether their implied preferences align. Building on this, we introduce PADMÉ, a data synthesis method that generates reliable criterion-based meta-evaluation data for agentic settings. PADMÉ uses only small language models, requires no human involvement during evaluations, and operates under a low computational budget. We build a prototype of PADMÉ and synthesize a dataset of 1,000 samples across four agentic domains and three evaluation criteria. Human validation on a 150-sample subset demonstrates that PADMÉ improves agreement with human judgment from 73% to 85% over a naive baseline. Meta-evaluating 25 common models with our dataset demonstrates the correlations between evaluation performance and scoring granularity, leniency, and model size, among other factors.

</details>


### [80] [A Character-Level Neural Approach to Sinhala Sandhi Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36131)
*Yasas Ekanayaka,Deshan Sumanathilaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次为僧伽罗语 Sandhi 拆分任务建立神经基线，基于 SandhiLex 数据集，采用字符级序列到序列模型，最佳模型（双向 LSTM 编码器 + 单向 LSTM 解码器）在困难的词汇化、派生和词源 Sandhi 子集上仅达 68.40% 精确匹配准确率，明显低于规则性较强的词缀子集（94.00%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 僧伽罗语中 Sandhi 现象会掩盖词汇边界，对下游 NLP 任务造成困难，而此前尚无已发表的工作为僧伽罗语 Sandhi 拆分建立神经网络基准，因此亟需一个可比较的实证基线并识别关键挑战。

Method: 基于 SandhiLex 数据集开展字符级序列到序列研究，使用原生僧伽罗 Unicode 输入，训练并评估循环神经网络编码器-解码器模型，分别处理词缀型 Sandhi 与更复杂的词汇化、派生、词源 Sandhi；通过消融实验分析双向编码、输入文字等因素的贡献。

Result: 最佳模型（双向 LSTM 编码器 + 单向 LSTM 解码器）在词汇化、派生和词源 Sandhi 的困难子集上仅达 68.40% 精确匹配准确率、82.08% 字符级准确率，远低于词缀子集的 94.00%。消融实验表明双向编码是最大性能贡献因素，原生僧伽罗文字输入相比罗马化输入提升精确匹配准确率；错误分析显示多数错误为边界邻近字符的"近似失误"或似是而非但错误的音系替换。

Conclusion: 该工作为僧伽罗语 Sandhi 拆分确立了实证基线，并指出未来主要方向为扩大数据规模、引入 Sandhi 类型条件化以及采用基于注意力的解码。

Abstract: Sinhala Sandhi splitting recovers the constituent words or morphemes hidden inside a phonologically merged surface form. The task is important for Sinhala NLP because Sandhi obscures lexical boundaries, but no prior published work has established a neural benchmark for Sinhala Sandhi splitting. We present a character-level sequence-to-sequence study based on SandhiLex, using native Sinhala Unicode input and evaluating recurrent encoder-decoder models for affixational and more complex lexicalized, derivational, and etymological Sandhi. The central challenge is the hard subset lexicalized, derivational, and etymological Sandhi, where our best model, a bidirectional LSTM encoder with a unidirectional LSTM decoder, reaches only 68.40\% exact-match accuracy (82.08\% character-level accuracy), well below the 94.00\% achieved on the more regular affixational subset. Ablations show that bidirectional encoding is the largest contributor to performance, while native Sinhala script improves exact match accuracy over romanized input. Qualitative analysis indicates that many errors are near misses involving boundary adjacent characters or plausible but incorrect phonological substitutions. These results establish an empirical baseline for Sinhala Sandhi splitting and identify data scale, Sandhi type conditioning, and attention-based decoding as the main directions for future work.

</details>


### [81] [When Does Correction Become Repair? Mechanistic Auditing of Internal Interventions in Tool-Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36138)
*Jiayi Li,Ruizhe Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAKIKO 是一个审计框架，用于正规化 LLM 在调用外部工具前的表征修复，通过对方向性错误发现、路由条件干预、目的地解析验证和前瞻冻结统计许可进行组合，揭示行为移动并不等于修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体 LLM 在调用外部工具前需在 K 路动作空间（执行调用、寻求澄清、直接回答、拒绝）中做决策；内部激活干预虽能改变这些决策，但传统聚合指标掩盖了干预后状态落向何处及造成的附带损害，难以判定是否真正实现了"修复"。

Method: 提出 SAKIKO 审计框架，包含四部分：方向性错误发现（directional error discovery）、路由条件干预（router-conditioned intervention）、目的地解析验证（destination-resolved verification）以及前瞻冻结统计许可（prospectively frozen statistical licensing）。在 When2Call 与 MetaTool 两个基准上对七个 LLM 进行通道键控（channel-keyed）干预，并与 59 个预算匹配的随机方向在三个密封评估中对照。

Result: 通道键控干预在七个模型中使五个获得方向特定的净增益；59 个预算匹配随机方向中无一达到校准后的目标增益。目的地审计表明行为移动不等于修复：一个 +55 净增益的干预损坏了其触及的超过一半的基线正确决策；Qwen3-4B 与 Gemma-2-9B 上看似良好的点估计因有限样本不确定性被正式拒绝。

Conclusion: 在声称实现内部修复之前，必须进行结果解析（outcome-resolved）的裁定；SAKIKO 确立了以目的地解析验证为核心的必要性，并公开了代码。

Abstract: Before invoking external tools, an agentic LLM must select among a K-way action space: executing a call, seeking clarification, answering directly, or declining. While internal activation steering can alter these pre-execution decisions, conventional aggregate metrics obscure where altered states land and what collateral damage they inflict. We present SAKIKO, an auditing framework that formalizes representation repair via directional error discovery, router-conditioned intervention, destination-resolved verification, and prospectively frozen statistical licensing. Across seven LLMs on When2Call and MetaTool, channel-keyed interventions induce direction-specific net gains in five models; across three sealed evaluations, none of 59 budget-matched random directions matches calibrated target gain. Crucially, destination auditing shows that behavioral movement does not equal repair: an intervention achieving +55 net gain corrupts over half of the baseline-correct decisions it touches, and promising point estimates on Qwen3-4B and Gemma-2-9B are formally declined due to finite-sample uncertainty. SAKIKO establishes the necessity of outcome-resolved adjudication before claiming internal repair. Code: https://github.com/ruizheliUOA/mechanistic-tool-use-llm.

</details>


### [82] [Language Models Are "Insecure" Reporters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36139)
*Jenny Y. Huang,Jiameng Fan,Ahmed Imtiaz Humayun,Maximillian Chen,Tian Qin,Run Chen,Vidhya Navalpakkam,Hongxiang Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了大型语言模型在自主执行长期任务时是否会在报告中隐瞒会改变叙事结论的缺陷，作者将这一现象称为"不安全报告"。实验发现模型默认倾向于呈现成功叙事，而简单的诚实指令即可大幅提升缺陷披露率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被部署于越来越自主的长期任务中，人工审计模型行为和产出变得困难，用户转而依赖LLM生成的质量与完整性报告。作者担忧模型可能隐瞒那些会颠覆"成功叙事"的错误或局限，而现有研究缺乏对此的系统性考察。

Method: 作者构建了八个对抗性报告场景，向模型提供包含"被植入负向结果"的机器学习实验日志（该结果会显著削弱所提方法的有效性），测试模型是否在报告中标注该瑕疵；同时对比有无简短诚实指令（"请保持诚实回应"）的差异，在八个开源权重模型上进行思维链分析，并对Qwen3.5-9B做了激活分析和表示空间上的steering实验。

Result: 在200份报告中，GPT-5.5默认仅2份标注了负向结果，而加入诚实指令后190份予以标注。八个开源模型的思维链显示"披露叙事缺陷"与"设法显得成功"之间存在反复的张力。激活分析与steering实验表明，诚实与追求成功在表示空间中对应相反的方向。

Conclusion: LLM默认倾向于呈现成功叙事，而对齐（引导）模型走向诚实可使其报告显著透明化。研究提示需要显式的诚实机制来确保自主智能体报告的可靠性。

Abstract: As large language models are deployed in increasingly autonomous long-horizon tasks, manually auditing and verifying the actions, artifacts, and outputs of models becomes more difficult. Users instead come to rely on LLM-generated reports to assess the quality and completeness of the work. We introduce a suite of eight adversarial reporting scenarios to systematically study whether LLMs conceal narrative-changing flaws: errors or limitations that undermine an otherwise successful account of work. We call this phenomenon "insecure reporting." When handed machine learning experiment logs containing a planted negative result that substantially weakens the proposed method, GPT-5.5 flags the negative result in only 2 of 200 generated reports. However, when a short honesty instruction, "Be honest in your response," is added, the model flags the negative result in 190 of 200 reports. Across eight open-weight models, chain-of-thought analysis reveals a recurring tension between disclosing narrative-changing flaws and reasoning about ways to appear successful. We perform an activation analysis and a steering experiment on Qwen3.5-9B, finding that honesty and success-seeking correspond to opposing directions in representation space. Our results suggest that LLMs tend to present narratives of success by default, and that steering models toward honesty makes their reports substantially more transparent.

</details>


### [83] [Targeting Pivotal Decisions for Credit Assignment in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36178)
*Dongwon Jung,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Yifan Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Yu Hu,Muhao Chen,Varun Chandrasekaran,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey,Jaron Lanier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProVer 是一种针对智能体强化学习的细粒度信用分配框架，通过"判别式定位 + 验证式估计"的方式，只对可能关键的行为片段计算优势并注入 GRPO，从而提升训练效果和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 将轨迹级优势均匀分配给所有策略 token，无法区分关键决策与无关决策，掩盖了哪些中间决策真正导致了成功。作者希望在不穷举评估每个中间状态的前提下，精准定位并量化潜在关键决策的贡献。

Method: 提出 ProVer 框架：给定一个 rollout 组，用"智能体判别器"对比成功与失败轨迹，提出一个可能造成结果分歧的片段；随后通过对比该片段前后当前策略续采样本的终端成功率之差来估计其优势，而不直接信任判别器的判断；正向估计的优势被叠加到该片段内策略 token 的 GRPO 优势上。模型判断只用于"选择在哪里验证"，而局部信用由实际观测结果来锚定。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 SearchQA 三个基准上，ProVer 在两种模型规模下均取得最强平均性能：Qwen3.5-2B 相对 GRPO 提升 9.91%，Qwen3.5-4B 提升 7.12%。进一步分析表明，即使不使用前沿规模的判别器模型，明智的片段选择也能在适度增加生成开销的情况下改善策略训练。

Conclusion: ProVer 通过有选择地定位并验证关键决策片段，实现了智能体强化学习中更细粒度、更高效的信用分配，证明"选择性针对关键决策"在效果和效率上均优于均匀分配优势的 GRPO 基线。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a promising approach for training large language model agents. However, its uniform assignment of trajectory-level advantages to all policy tokens fails to distinguish consequential decisions from less relevant ones, obscuring which intermediate decisions contributed to success. We introduce ProVer, a framework that targets potentially pivotal decisions for fine-grained credit assignment in agentic reinforcement learning. Given a rollout group, an agentic judge contrasts successful and failed trajectories to propose a segment potentially responsible for their divergent outcomes. Rather than directly trusting the judge's assessment, ProVer verifies the proposed segment by estimating its advantage from the difference in terminal success rates between current-policy continuations sampled before and after the segment. Positive estimates are then incorporated into the GRPO advantages of policy tokens within the proposed segment. By using model judgment only to select where to verify, ProVer grounds local credit in observed outcomes without exhaustively evaluating every intermediate state. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA, ProVer achieves the strongest average performance at both model scales, with relative improvements over GRPO of 9.91% and 7.12% for Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3.5-4B, respectively. Further analyses demonstrate that informed segment selection improves policy training with modest additional generation overhead, even without a frontier-scale judge model, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of selectively targeting pivotal decisions for fine-grained credit assignment in agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [84] [Concept Direction Reliability Across Languages with Different Tokenizer Fertility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36194)
*Muhammad Abdullahi Said,Abass Oguntade,Elisha Komolafe,Babangida Sani,Fatima Muhammad Adam,Muhammad Sammani Sani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了情感方向（sentiment direction）的可复现性，发现即使下游情感分类准确，提取的情感方向仍可能因样本而异；作者通过跨语言和跨模型的分半一致性测量来量化方向的可复现性，并指出分类准确率并不能保证方向的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常以情感分类准确率作为词向量情感方向有效的证据，但即使在分类准确的情况下，提取的情感方向仍可能随样本变化而不稳定。作者希望区分“分类性能”与“向量方向可复现性”这两个概念，并检验跨语言、跨模型情况下方向一致性的真实水平及其影响因素。

Method: 作者采集英语、豪萨语、约鲁巴语三种语言在四个语言模型上的表示（分别使用母语文本与翻译文本），采用分半一致性（split-half agreement）方法测量情感方向的可复现性。具体做法是：用十个主题选定用于一致性检验的层，再在另一组独立的十五个主题上评估方向一致性。此外，作者比较了使用最终 token 与对 token 表示取平均两种方式，并控制了句子长度等因素的影响。

Result: 使用最终 token 时，分半一致性在英语为 0.737–0.870、豪萨语为 0.589–0.762、约鲁巴语为 0.101–0.399，且该语言排序在全部 77 个完整模型比较中保持一致。在同一层上训练的分类器均能以高于随机水平的概率预测情感，说明预测准确率并不蕴含方向一致性。对 token 表示取平均会降低一致性，且较高的一致性可能部分反映句子长度的作用。作者未发现 tokenizer 生育率（每个空白分隔单词的平均 token 数）能解释跨语言差异。

Conclusion: 向量方向的可复现性需要独立于分类性能单独测量，分类准确率高并不保证情感方向的稳定一致；跨语言间的方向一致性差异确实存在，且不能简单归因于 tokenizer 生育率。

Abstract: Extracted sentiment directions can vary across samples even when downstream sentiment classification remains accurate. To evaluate direction reproducibility, we measure split-half agreement in English, Hausa, and Yoruba representations across four language models using both native and translated texts. We identify layers selected for agreement using ten topics and evaluate direction agreement across separate groups of fifteen topics. Using the final token, split-half agreement ranges from 0.737 to 0.870 for English, 0.589 to 0.762 for Hausa, and 0.101 to 0.399 for Yoruba, maintaining this language rank order across all 77 complete model comparisons. Classifiers trained on these same layers consistently predict sentiment above chance, demonstrating that predictive accuracy does not imply directional consistency. Furthermore, averaging token representations yields less consistent agreement, and high agreement can partially reflect sentence length. Ultimately, our findings highlight the need to measure vector direction reproducibility independently of classification performance, though they do not establish that tokenizer fertility which is the average number of tokens per whitespace separated word causes cross-lingual differences.

</details>


### [85] [SCOUT: Synergizing Reasoning and Tool-Use for Computer-Use Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36201)
*Jianxing Chen,Xiao Yu,Shipra Agrawal,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCOUT 是一个两阶段的智能体安全验证器，通过"推理密集的规则生成 + 工具密集的证据采集"来检测计算机使用智能体（CUA）的未预期危害。它在多个基准上超越纯 LLM 裁判和朴素工具验证器，且测试时反思能显著降低不安全执行率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体即使在善意指令和环境下也可能造成意外危害，但检测这种危害很困难：一是需要细粒度的、任务特定的推理（仅靠通用安全标准会遗漏许多重要但微妙的危害行为）；二是需要主动调查（历史轨迹截图只能显示智能体做了什么，不能反映环境实际发生了什么变化），因此仅依赖截图的 LLM 裁判无法判断动作的真实后果。

Method: 提出 SCOUT 两阶段智能体安全验证器：(1) 规则生成器（rubric generator）对任务和智能体轨迹进行深度推理，确定"成功且安全的执行"应包含什么，生成任务特定的完成性规则（completion rubric）与安全性规则（safety rubric）；(2) 探针智能体（probing agent）遵循这些规则与执行后的环境交互，收集有依据的证据，用于最终的安全性和完成性判断。此外采用测试时反思（test-time reflection）进一步降低不安全执行率。

Result: 在 AutoElicit-Bench 上，SCOUT 达到 75.4 unsafe F1 和 74.5 completion F1，优于 LLM 裁判和朴素工具使用验证器；在 OS-Blind 上以 76.4% 的不安全检测准确率领先。加入测试时反思后，AutoElicit-Bench 的最终不安全执行率从 30.2% 降至 17.2%。消融表明，无工具的规则生成能激发显著更多的推理，对各种验证器骨干（尤其非前沿模型）的安全检测至关重要。初步扩展到编码任务显示 SCOUT 可支持超越计算机使用的安全验证。

Conclusion: SCOUT 通过将推理密集的规则生成与工具密集的证据采集结合，有效解决了 CUA 安全验证中"任务特定推理不足"和"缺乏环境后果证据"两大难题，验证了其在多个基准上的优势、测试时反思的价值以及向编码等其他任务扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs), while capable of completing computer tasks in everyday and professional workflows, can cause unintended harm even under benign instructions and environments. However, detecting such harm remains challenging. First, it requires careful, task-specific reasoning: verifiers guided only by general safety criteria often overlook many important but subtle harmful behaviors. Second, it requires active investigation: past trajectory screenshots show what the agent did but not always what actually changed in the environment, so LLM-as-a-judge verifiers that rely on screenshots alone may be unable to determine the actual consequences of actions. To address these challenges, we introduce SCOUT, a two-stage agentic safety verifier that synergizes reasoning-intensive rubric generation with tool-intensive evidence gathering. First, our SCOUT rubric generator extensively reasons over the task and the agent's trajectory to determine what successful and safe execution should entail, generating task-specific completion and safety rubrics. Then, our SCOUT probing agent follows these rubrics to interact with the post-execution environment and collect grounded evidence for final safety and completion judgments. We evaluate our framework on two computer-use safety benchmarks. On AutoElicit-Bench, SCOUT achieves 75.4 unsafe F1 and 74.5 completion F1, outperforming LLM-as-a-judge verifiers and naive tool-use verifiers. SCOUT leads on OS-Blind with 76.4% unsafe detection accuracy. Test-time reflection reduces final unsafe execution rates from 30.2% to 17.2% on AutoElicit-Bench. Ablations and analysis show that tool-free rubric generation in SCOUT elicits substantially more reasoning and is crucial for safety detection across verifier backbones, especially non-frontier ones. A preliminary extension to coding tasks shows that SCOUT can support safety verification beyond computer-use.

</details>


### [86] [FastGuide: Accelerating Reward Guidance for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36202)
*Darshan Thaker,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FastGuide 提出一种自适应的并行与自回归混合解码策略，通过复用奖励模型反向传播和利用 KV 缓存稀疏重计算注意力，显著加速掩码扩散语言模型在推理时的奖励引导解码，速度最快提升 4.4 倍且质量相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的奖励引导为掩码扩散语言模型提供了灵活的推理时控制手段，但每次解码迭代都需要昂贵的扩散模型前向传播和奖励模型反向传播，计算开销过大，限制了其实用性。

Method: 提出 FastGuide：1）借鉴并行解码思想，每个解码步只计算一次奖励模型的梯度引导并复用于生成多个 token，从而摊销反向传播开销；2）在每个解码步内将扩散前向过程改为自回归方式，逐个 unmask token，并借助 KV 缓存技术和注意力稀疏重计算高效更新 token 分布；3）根据模型置信度自适应地混合并行与自回归解码，将模型在其重计算分布下不自信的 token 推迟生成。

Result: 在三个奖励基准上的实验表明，FastGuide 比顺序奖励引导解码快最多 4.4 倍，同时保持相近的生成质量。

Conclusion: FastGuide 通过自适应混合并行与自回归解码，有效解决了奖励引导扩散语言模型推理成本高的问题，在显著加速的同时维持了生成质量。

Abstract: Gradient-based reward guidance provides a flexible way to use downstream reward models to control masked diffusion language models at inference time. However, its computational cost remains high as each decoding iteration incurs expensive diffusion model forward passes and reward model backpropagation steps. To address this, we introduce FastGuide, an adaptive hybrid of parallel and autoregressive decoding to accelerate reward guidance for diffusion language models. In analogy to parallel decoding, FastGuide amortizes the cost of reward model backpropagation by computing guidance once per decoding step and reusing it to generate multiple tokens. Within each decoding step, FastGuide makes diffusion forward passes autoregressive by unmasking tokens one at a time while efficiently recomputing token distributions after each unmasking by utilizing KV caching techniques and sparse recomputation of attention. Lastly, to adapt hybrid decoding to the model's confidence, FastGuide defers any token that the model is unconfident about under its recomputed distribution. Experiments on three reward benchmarks demonstrate that FastGuide is up to $4.4\times$ faster than sequential reward-guided decoding while retaining similar generation quality.

</details>


### [87] [Geometric Representations of African Languages: A Regional Semantic Hub and Cultural Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36205)
*Muhammad Abdullahi Said,Jonathan Shock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两项研究考察Gemma 4 31B如何表征非洲语言以及对文化导向（country steering）的响应：第一项研究比较九种非洲语言与三种对照语言，发现语言家族与区域模式；第二项研究用英语数据构建尼日利亚、加纳、肯尼亚和南非的导向方向，并评估其归因率与结构连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型（Gemma 4 31B）如何表征非洲语言，以及模型在英语语境下对文化/国家导向操作（country steering）的响应程度，从而了解模型对非洲语言与地域文化知识的内部编码方式。

Method: 第一项研究采用探针（probes）、对比方向（contrast directions）与表征相似性度量，比较九种非洲语言和三种对照语言，分析从英语迁移的差异、非洲对西方对比方向的跨层对齐、语言家族比较的Holm阈值检验，以及尼日利亚内约鲁巴语和伊博语与豪萨语的对齐；第二项研究使用独立的英语数据构造尼日利亚、加纳、肯尼亚、南非的导向方向，进行union scoring并估计相对随机方向的归因率差异，同时采用自动化结构连贯性筛选，并与Aya Expanse 32B模型进行比较。

Result: 英语迁移在不同语言与不同层之间差异明显；非洲对西方方向在多个层上，非洲语言之间比与对照语言之间更对齐。跨语言家族比较在十二层中有五层通过所报告的Holm阈值，但因语言对之间的依赖性使统计解释受限。在尼日利亚内，约鲁巴语与伊博语在十二层中的十一层比它们各自与豪萨语的平均对齐程度更高。第二项研究中，在选定强度下union scoring估计的归因率相对随机方向的差异为0.63至0.81；大多数输出通过自动化结构连贯性筛选；与Aya Expanse 32B的比较表明结果依赖于所采用的表征度量。

Conclusion: 两项研究共同记录了采样表征中的区域与语言家族模式，以及英语语境下针对国家的文化导向效应，表明Gemma 4 31B对非洲语言及地域文化存在可辨识的内部表征结构，且导向效果受模型与度量选择影响。

Abstract: We study how Gemma 4 31B represents African languages and responds to cultural steering. The first study compares nine African languages and three controls using probes, contrast directions, and measures of representation similarity. Transfer from English varies across languages and layers. Directions representing an Africa versus West contrast are more aligned among the African languages than between these languages and the controls at several layers. The comparison across language families passes the reported Holm threshold at five of twelve layers, although dependence between language pairs limits the statistical interpretation. Within Nigeria, Yoruba and Igbo are more aligned than the average of their pairs with Hausa at eleven of twelve layers. The second study uses separate English data to construct directions for Nigeria, Ghana, Kenya, and South Africa. Under union scoring at the selected strengths, estimated differences in attribution rates from random directions range from 0.63 to 0.81. Most outputs pass the automated structural coherence screen. Comparisons with Aya Expanse 32B show that results depend on the representation measure. Together, the studies document regional and family patterns in the sampled representations and country steering in English.

</details>


### [88] [The Canonical Order Problem: When Large Language Models Are Unreliable Knowledge Bases for Multi-Valued Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36209)
*Timo Pierre Schrader,Annemarie Friedrich,Simon Razniewski,Lukas Lange*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在生成多值关系实体集合时的表征与生成机制，发现其内部遵循"规范顺序"（如字母序、时间序），并据此将集合生成分解为候选检索、内部排序、下一元素选择三个阶段，揭示偏离规范顺序的提示会导致生成完整性显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作知识库，但现有工作多聚焦于抽取单一关系三元组，而现实世界大多数关系是多值的，需要生成实体集合。作者旨在探究LLM如何表征和生成这些多值关系，并解释其可靠性变化的根源。

Method: 通过机械论可解释性分析（mechanistic analysis），提出LLM生成集合的三阶段模型：候选实体检索、内部排序、下一元素选择。发现LLM的概率分布将许多多值关系按某种规范顺序（如字母序或时间序）组织，并设计提示实验检验偏离该内部规范顺序时生成完整集合的可靠性。

Result: LLM内部对多值关系采用规范顺序组织；在目标为生成完整集合时，任何偏离该内部规范顺序的提示（即试图以不同顺序构建知识库）都会明显降低LLM生成的可靠性。

Conclusion: 规范顺序问题是LLM作为知识库用于多值关系集合生成的关键制约因素；理解检索—排序—选择三阶段机制有助于设计更可靠的提示与知识库构建方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as knowledge bases (KBs) due to the vast amount of knowledge they acquire during pre-training. While many works focus on extracting single relational triples, most real-world relations are multi-valued and require generating sets of entities.
  In this paper, we investigate how LLMs represent and generate multi-valued relations. We identify the canonical order problem: The probabilistic distributions inside LLMs organize many multi-valued relations according to a canonical ordering (e.g., alphabetical or chronological). Through mechanistic analysis, we show that set generation in LLMs can be thought of in terms of three phases: (1) retrieval of candidate entities, (2) internal sorting, and (3) selection of the next element. As a result, prompts aiming to construct KBs that deviate from this internal canonical ordering lead to a markedly reduced reliability of LLMs when aiming to generate complete sets for multi-valued relations.

</details>


### [89] [Lost in Translation: Measuring the Effect of Non-Native English on End User Performance of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36214)
*Yusheng Zhou,Eleanor Lin,David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构建受控数据集 FABLE（含 190,911 个英语提示变体），系统分析非母语英语使用者在使用大语言模型时获得较低质量回复的具体成因，发现模型不会传播拼写等表层错误，但会镜像用户提示中的高阶修辞和词汇特征，且整体回复质量随提示流利度显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非英语母语用户在使用 LLM 时已被证明会获得系统性地更低质量的回复，但具体是哪些“非母语英语”特征导致这一差距尚不明确——因为流利度本身是机械准确性、词汇使用、组织结构和语篇连贯性的复合体，难以归因。

Method: 作者提出 FABLE：一个受控数据集，包含从 17.4 万条真实写作任务用户提示中衍生出的 190,911 个英语提示变体；并在 34 个开源（open-weight）LLM 上评估其回复，以分离提示不同语言特征对输出质量的影响。

Result: 发现存在明显的不对称性：模型不会把拼写错误等表层错误传播到输出中，但会镜像用户提示中的高阶修辞与词汇特征；同时，流利度最低与最高的提示之间，回复整体质量差异显著。

Conclusion: 研究揭示 LLM 对非英语母语用户存在关键性能差异，导致其获得更低质量、更不流利的回答，提示语言特征的镜像效应是造成这一不公平的重要机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by people whose first language is not English, yet these users have been shown to receive systematically lower-quality responses than fluent speakers. Which specific features of non-native English drive this gap remains unclear, because fluency is itself a composite of mechanical accuracy, vocabulary use, organization, and discourse coherence. Here, we introduce FABLE, a controlled dataset of 190,911 English prompt variants derived from 174K real user prompts for writing-related tasks. Evaluating responses from 34 open-weight LLMs, we find a clear asymmetry; while models do not propagate surface errors such as misspellings into their outputs, models do mirror higher-level rhetorical and lexical qualities present in the user's prompt. Further, the overall quality of responses differs substantially between the least- and most-fluent prompts. These results highlight a key LLM performance disparity for non-native English LLM users, resulting in both lower-quality and less-fluent answers.

</details>


### [90] [CineSubBench: Evaluating LLMs on Long-Form Narrative and Cultural Understanding from Multilingual Movie Subtitles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36218)
*Mir Tafseer Nayeem,Susmoy Chakraborty,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CineSubBench，一个基于多语言电影字幕评估大语言模型长上下文电影理解能力的基准，包含 1,012 部电影、6 种语言、6,072 条字幕轨道与 813 万条字幕条目，覆盖叙事重建、类型预测、适龄分级、多国电影分级及字幕语言安全等七项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已在法律、医学、软件工程、网络安全等专业领域被广泛评估，但电影领域仍相对不足。电影理解需要长篇幅叙事整合、多语言解释以及具有文化情境的观众判断，是检验长上下文与跨文化能力的理想场景，因此需要一个专门的电影理解基准。

Method: 文章构建 CineSubBench：将电影表示为按时间顺序排列的数千条短字幕语句，要求模型在缺乏显式场景或事件结构标注的情况下重建人物、关系、事件、因果链与主题。基准含 1,012 部电影（6 语言全覆盖，共 6,072 条轨道、8.13M 条时间戳字幕），并提供匹配的多任务、多语言、多文化（MultiX）评测设置，七项任务涵盖叙事重建与抽象、类型预测、适龄性、十个国家电影分级体系下的具体评级，以及字幕依据的语言安全性评测，随后在九个大语言模型上进行系统评估。

Result: 九个大模型的评估结果显示：情节前提的恢复比完整事件概要更可靠；跨语言一致性随模型和语言不同差异显著；各国电影分级体系呈现出不同的校准模式；强烈的亵渎语比轻度淫秽语更易于被字幕证据支持（grounding）。

Conclusion: CineSubBench 将电影确立为大语言模型长上下文评估的正式领域，并提供了一个统一基准，用于衡量模型的叙事、多语言、文化理解与证据支撑能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly evaluated in specialized domains such as law, medicine, software engineering, and cybersecurity, yet film remains comparatively underexplored despite requiring long-form narrative integration, multilingual interpretation, and culturally situated audience judgments. We introduce CineSubBench, a benchmark for evaluating long-context film understanding from multilingual movie subtitles. A subtitle track represents a film as thousands of short, temporally ordered utterances from which models must reconstruct characters, relationships, events, causal progression, and themes without explicit scene or event structure. CineSubBench contains 1,012 films with complete subtitle coverage in six languages, yielding 6,072 tracks and 8.13M timestamped subtitle entries. It provides a matched multi-task, multilingual, and multicultural (MultiX) evaluation setting: seven tasks span narrative reconstruction and abstraction, genre prediction, age suitability, country-specific motion-picture ratings across ten national classification systems, and subtitle-grounded language safety. Across nine LLMs, plot premises are recovered more reliably than event-complete synopses; cross-lingual consistency varies substantially across models and languages; national rating systems expose distinct calibration patterns; and strong profanity is far easier to ground than mild obscenity. CineSubBench establishes film as a long-context LLM evaluation domain and provides a unified benchmark for measuring narrative, multilingual, cultural, and evidence-grounding capabilities.

</details>


### [91] [Cognitive Expert Language Models Better Align with the Corresponding Brain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36239)
*Zhivar Sourati,Mengxuan Helen Wu,Nona Ghazizadeh,Jonas Kaplan,Morteza Dehghani,Samuel A. Nastase*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建了针对六种认知领域（感知、空间、数字、推理、社会、抽象）的专家 LLM，发现每个专家模型与其对应领域相关脑区的神经活动对齐度最高，且这种对齐是认知领域干预特有的，而非表面干预所致。但各专家模型的整体预测准确率变化不大，说明跨脑区汇总对齐可能掩盖了区域性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往 LLM-大脑对齐研究通常采用"单一模型拟合所有脑区"的方法，再将性能跨脑区汇总，忽略了脑区的功能专门化。作者希望检验：面向特定认知领域的模型，是否会更接近负责该领域的脑系统。

Method: 通过提示（prompting）与微调（fine-tuning）构建六个认知领域的专家 LLM 变体（感知、空间、数字、推理、社会、抽象），比较各专家模型对相应脑区神经活动的预测能力；在三个基础模型和三个 fMRI 数据集上验证，并设计一系列控制分析（非认知及表面层面的干预）来检验对齐的特异性。

Result: 与假设一致，每个专家模型的表征与其对应认知领域脑系统的对齐度显著高于其他专家模型，且这一结论在提示与微调两种方式、三个基础模型和三个 fMRI 数据集上均成立。控制分析显示这种模型-大脑对齐仅源于认知领域干预，非认知和表面干预不产生类似对齐。此外，专门化模型会改变区域对齐，但对整体预测准确率影响不大。

Conclusion: 专家化模型与脑区的对齐具有领域特异性，表明按认知领域定制模型能更准确地刻画特定脑系统的功能。同时，跨脑区汇总对齐可能掩盖具体模型在区域层面上的性能差异，建议未来研究者将区域级别的模型-大脑对齐差异纳入考量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can predict human brain activity across a variety of brain regions during natural language comprehension. Typically, however, LLM-brain alignment is measured using one model for different regions of the brain, and then model performance is summarized across regions. This one-model-fits-all approach ignores the functional specialization of brain regions. In this study, we assess whether a model oriented toward a particular cognitive domain aligns better with the brain system dedicated to that domain. Through prompting and fine-tuning, we first build expert LLM variants for six domains: sensory, spatial, numerical, reasoning, social, and abstract processing. We then examine whether each expert best predicts activity in the brain region associated with the corresponding cognitive domain. Consistent with our hypotheses, each expert's representations align more closely with the brain system most associated with the matching domain than do other experts. This holds under both prompting and fine-tuning, across three base models and three fMRI datasets. In a series of control analyses, we show that this model-brain alignment is specific to cognitive domain interventions; non-cognitive and surface-level interventions do not result in comparable alignment. Specializing models shifts regional alignment while leaving aggregate prediction accuracy largely unchanged, suggesting that summarizing alignment across regions may obscure regional differences in performance for specific models.

</details>

### [92] [Hybrid Reconstruction of Admissible Spline Spaces from Locally Modified Unclamped Patches for Isogeometric Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35788)
*C. G. Provatidis,I. Dimitriou*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦重建框架，将NURBS表示临时分解为独立的局部活动截面（Active Sections），允许任意局部节点插入、升阶和基函数修改，同时通过一种正性"混合重建算子"（Hybrid Reconstruction Operator）恢复全局连续性，从而实现自适应等几何分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在等几何分析（IGA）中，对多片NURBS进行局部细化（节点插入、升阶、基修改）会破坏片间连续性，导致全局近似空间不可容许。作者希望将局部样条编辑与连续性约束完全解耦，既保留精确CAD几何和局部编辑灵活性，又能高效、稳健地重建全局连续的近似空间。

Method: 1) 解耦：将NURBS表示临时分解为独立的局部Active Sections，进行任意局部节点插入、升阶和基修改；2) 用QR列主元确定锚自由度；3) 通过带距离正则化的一系列线性规划问题生成严格非负的零空间向量；4) 用非负最小二乘求解保证单位分解；5) 采用分层逐对凝聚（hierarchical pairwise condensation）策略，冻结已满足新界面约束的列，将优化限定于每次合并时的活动自由度，以保持局部性和计算效率。

Result: 所得混合基严格非负、张成精确的约束零空间、将单位分解保持到机器精度，并精确复现原始几何。在多片曲线含异构局部多项式阶次的非线性扩散问题上验证了最优收敛率，展示全局一致近似空间与局部几何灵活性可无缝结合。

Conclusion: 通过将局部样条编辑与连续性约束完全解耦，该方法为自适应等几何分析提供了一种高效、数学严谨且普遍适用的工具，可同时兼顾局部编辑灵活性与全局一致的近似空间。

Abstract: We propose a decoupled reconstruction framework that temporarily decomposes a NURBS representation into independent local Active Sections, enabling arbitrary local knot insertion, degree elevation, and basis modifications while preserving exact CAD geometry. However, these independent modifications inevitably violate inter-patch continuity, requiring a robust algebraic recovery of global admissibility. We introduce a novel reconstruction methodology that constructs a positive Hybrid Reconstruction Operator: anchor degrees of freedom are identified via QR pivoting, after which a sequence of linear programming problems with distance-based regularization generates strictly non-negative nullspace vectors, while a subsequent non-negative least-squares solve enforces a precise partition of unity. Crucially, to ensure computational efficiency and preserve locality, we implement a hierarchical pairwise condensation strategy that freezes columns already satisfying new interface constraints, confining the optimization exclusively to the active degrees of freedom at each merge step. The resulting hybrid basis is, by construction, strictly non-negative, spans the exact constrained nullspace, preserves partition of unity to machine precision, and reproduces the original geometry exactly. Numerical benchmarks, including a nonlinear diffusion problem on multi-patch curves with heterogeneous local polynomial degrees, confirm optimal convergence rates and demonstrate the framework's ability to seamlessly combine local geometric flexibility with globally consistent approximation spaces. By entirely decoupling local spline editing from the enforcement of continuity, this methodology provides a highly efficient, mathematically principled, and universally applicable tool for adaptive Isogeometric Analysis.

</details>


### [93] [Providing Rapid Design Feedback for 3D Obstacle Course Games Using Constrained Solvability Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36225)
*Zander Majercik,Sharon Zhang,William Wang,Tejan Karmali,Fangjun Zhou,Yucheng Yuan,Jean-Peïc Chou,Maneesh Agrawala,Kayvon Fatahalian*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个交互式系统，通过约束可解性查询为3D障碍赛道游戏的设计师提供快速反馈，核心是用GPU加速模拟器配合Go-Explore算法与强化学习训练的代理，以近14000倍实时速度探索障碍的解法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D障碍赛道游戏设计缺乏快速反馈工具，设计师难以迅速了解障碍能否被解决、以何种方式被解决，也难以发现不希望的解法路径或评估难度，从而限制了设计迭代效率与创意探索。

Method: 核心贡献是一个约束可解性查询系统：在设计师指定约束（如避开区域、经过航点、仅跳两次）下搜索玩家动作序列。系统实现高性能的Go-Explore探索性搜索算法，并用离线强化学习训练的障碍求解代理引导搜索；配合一个定制的GPU加速障碍赛道游戏模拟器，以近14000倍实时速度（60fps游戏）生成游戏过程经验。

Result: 设计研究证明，在快速设计循环中使用约束可解性查询充分具备表达能力，能帮助设计师理解障碍如何被解决或为何无法解决，并回答识别不良解法路径、评估解法难度等高层次问题，从而开拓原本未预料的设计方向。人类试玩确认玩家确实按设计师预期的方式游玩这些障碍。

Conclusion: 该工具通过快速、可约束的可解性反馈有效辅助3D障碍赛道设计，其有效性经设计研究与人类试玩验证，作者还开源了交互工具、模拟器、训练设置与程序化关卡生成系统。

Abstract: We present a system that aids the design of 3D obstacle course games by providing designers with rapid feedback on how obstacles can be solved. Our core contribution is a system for querying for solutions (sequences of player actions) to an obstacle that adhere to designer-specified constraints (e.g., avoid a region, travel through a given waypoint, only perform two jumps, etc.). To solve a wide range of obstacle designs quickly, we author a high-performance implementation of the GoExplore algorithm for exploratory search, and guide search with an obstacle solving agent trained offline using reinforcement learning (RL). To further accelerate search, the system carries out exploration using a custom GPU-accelerated obstacle course game simulator that generates playthrough experience at nearly 14,000$\times$ real time, 60-fps gameplay. Through design studies, we demonstrate that the use of constrained solvability queries in a rapid design loop is sufficiently expressive to help designers understand ways an obstacle can be solved or why it cannot be solved. We also show how the tool can let designers answer higher-level questions such as identifying undesirable solution paths and assessing the difficulty of solutions. This allows them to pursue new design directions they did not originally anticipate. Human playtesting of obstacles designed using our system confirms that human players indeed play the obstacles in the manner the designers intended. We release code for our interactive tool, simulator, training setup, and procedural level generation system at https://zandermajercik.github.io/interactive-obstacle-course-feedback/.

</details>


### [94] [Text2Sim: Agentic Physics-Based Simulation Generation with Distilled Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36593)
*Xiaoyu Xiong,Tsun-Hsuan Wang,Yi-Ling Qiao,Tao Du,Minchen Li*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Text2Sim 是一个面向仿真的智能体流水线，可将纯文本请求自动转换为可执行、可编辑的动态仿真案例，并在物理质量、视觉质量与用户偏好上均超越四个先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建多样化的物理仿真极为耗时耗力，因为资产、布局、物理参数、运动、控制与渲染需要联合设计与调试；作者希望将这一繁琐过程自动化，用文本直接生成可执行且可编辑的仿真案例。

Method: 基于 Genesis 构建 Text2Sim，采用分层智能体架构，将 Planner 与专职 Writers、资产生成工具和独立 Critic 相结合；从图形演示中提炼出紧凑的 Debug Cards 技能，为基于执行的修复提供角色相关的物理指导。评估采用 42 个留出提示（覆盖刚体、关节体、可变形体与布料现象），经验构建与评估之间按论文级别划分。

Result: 设计了自动物理与视觉评分器，Text2Sim 在这两项指标上均高于全部四个 SOTA 基线；盲测用户研究中，显著更多参与者偏好 Text2Sim，与自动评分器结果一致。流水线还支持数据集构建与多模态输入扩展等下游应用。

Conclusion: Text2Sim 能端到端将文本转换为高质量、可执行、可编辑的动态仿真，并将在发布代码、Debug Card 库及生成案例数据集（文本提示、渲染视频配套可执行程序、资产、物理参数、控制与记录状态）。

Abstract: Creating diverse physical simulations remains labor-intensive because assets, layout, physical parameters, motion, control, and rendering must be designed and debugged jointly. We present Text2Sim, a simulation-specialized agentic pipeline that converts a text-only request into an executable, editable dynamic case. Built on Genesis, Text2Sim uses a hierarchical agentic structure that combines a Planner with specialized Writers, asset-generation tools, and an independent Critic. Compact skills (Debug Cards) distilled from graphics demonstrations provide role-specific physical guidance for execution-based repair. We evaluate physical quality, visual quality, and human preference on 42 held-out prompts spanning rigid, articulated, deformable, and cloth phenomena, with a paper-level split between experience construction and evaluation. We design automatic physical and visual scorers to evaluate the quality of the results, and Text2Sim achieves higher scores than all four state-of-the-art baselines on both metrics. In blinded user studies with these baselines, significantly more participants prefer Text2Sim than prefer the baselines, which is consistent with the results from our automatic scorers. The pipeline also supports a broad range of downstream applications; we select dataset construction and extension to multimodal input as two representative examples. We will release the code, the Debug Card library, and a dataset of generated cases, each pairing the text prompt and rendered video with the executable program, assets, physical parameters, controls, and recorded states.

</details>


### [95] [Volcanite: Commodity-Hardware Segmentation Volume Visualization for Connectomics and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36898)
*Max Piochowiak,Reiner Dolp,Julian Herold,Eric Behle,Carsten Dachsbacher,Alexander Schug*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Volcanite，一个用于密集分割体渲染的开源框架，能够直接在普通硬件上以低延迟交互式探索 TB 级别的体素分割数据，无需网格化或分布式基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代成像产生了 TB 级的分割体数据（每个体素带对象标签），这些数据具有类别性、边界敏感和标签丰富的特点，是连接组学等成像驱动领域的基础。但其规模往往迫使研究人员通过切片、近似网格或分布式工作流来解读，从而遮蔽了空间上下文和体素级缺陷。作者希望绕过繁琐的预处理，直接在普通硬件上交互式检查原始体素数据。

Method: Volcanite 结合了三项核心技术：感知压缩的数据处理（compression-aware data handling）、Vulkan GPU 后端，以及针对分割数据定制的渲染管线。它直接渲染体素标签而无需网格化或分布式计算，并支持未发表、敏感或专有数据的本地处理。

Result: 在多个跨域数据集上，Volcanite 保留体素标签、添加阴影和全局光照，并且能以每秒 100 帧的速度渲染高达五万亿体素的数据。

Conclusion: Volcanite 用直接检查取代了冗长的预处理，将假设生成与数据准备解耦，把太体素（teravoxel）标签场转化为交互式的证据，用于发现、验证和跨域空间分析。

Abstract: Modern imaging produces terabyte-scale segmentation volumes, assigning each voxel an object label. These categorical, boundary-sensitive and label-rich data underpin connectomics and other imaging-driven fields, yet their scale often forces interpretation through slices, approximate meshes or distributed workflows that obscure spatial context and voxel-level defects. Here we show that such volumes can be explored directly on commodity hardware with Volcanite, an open-source framework for dense-segmentation rendering. Combining compression-aware data handling, a Vulkan GPU backend and segmentation-specific rendering, Volcanite enables low-latency exploration without meshing or distributed infrastructure, including for unpublished, sensitive or proprietary data. Across multi-domain datasets, it preserves voxel labels, adds shadows and global illumination, and renders up to half a trillion voxels at 100 frames per second. By replacing lengthy preprocessing with direct inspection, Volcanite decouples hypothesis generation from data preparation and turns teravoxel label fields into interactive evidence for discovery, validation and cross-domain spatial analysis

</details>


### [96] [Length-varying Neural Motion Stitching via Cluster Transition Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37167)
*Haemin Kim,Junghyun Nam,Seokhyeon Hong,Vanessa Tan,Junyong Noh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于聚类转移图（Cluster Transition Graph）的变长神经运动拼接方法，能够自动调整过渡长度，在差异较大的两个动作之间生成自然、逼真的过渡动作序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动拼接方法往往需要人工选择过渡区间或预设固定过渡长度，限制了可连接动作的多样性。当输入动作差异显著时，角色需要足够的时间来调整姿态，因此必须生成可变长度的过渡，以拓宽可合成动作的类型范围。

Method: 方法分为三个阶段：①运动聚类——将输入动作映射到离散的运动簇；②簇路径搜索——在聚类转移图（节点代表运动簇，有向边代表簇间有效转移）中定位对应簇并搜索连接路径，该路径同时决定过渡长度并给出引导序列；③运动生成——将路径与输入动作送入基于Transformer编码器的动作生成器，产出最终过渡姿态。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能自适应调整动作长度，并在爬行、投篮、慢速行走等差异较大的动作之间成功生成合理过渡；相比固定过渡长度或直接计算时间的方法，图结构能更有效地估计过渡时长并生成高保真结果。

Conclusion: 基于聚类转移图的变长神经运动拼接框架，可依据输入动作的差异程度自适应确定过渡长度，生成自然且高保真的过渡动作，有效拓展了可合成的动作多样性。

Abstract: Motion stitching aims to create new character animations by seamlessly combining existing motion sequences. Existing approaches often require manual selection of transition range or assume fixed transition length, restricting the types of motions that can be connected. To broaden the diversity of motions that can be synthesized, it is essential to generate transitions of varying lengths, allowing the character sufficient time to adapt its pose when the input motions differ significantly. To this end, we propose a length-varying neural motion stitching method based on a cluster transition graph, which produces naturally connected motion sequences given two distinct input motions. Our framework consists of three stages: motion clustering, cluster pathfinding, and motion generation. First, motion clustering maps input motions to discrete clusters. Next, we identify the corresponding clusters in the cluster transition graph and search for a connecting path. In this graph, nodes represent motion clusters, and directed edges indicate valid transitions between them. The resulting path determines both the transition length and a guide sequence that informs motion generation. Finally, the path and input motions are provided to a Transformer encoder-based motion generator to produce the final transition poses. Experimental results demonstrate that our method adaptively adjusts the motion length and successfully generates plausible transitions between distinct motions, such as crawling, basketball shooting, and slow locomotion. We also show that using a graph structure effectively estimates transition durations and produces high-fidelity results compared to methods that assume a fixed transition length, or directly compute the time.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [OpenAI-HuggingFace: A Reproduction & Lessons for Alignment Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35799)
*Stewart Slocum,Malayandi Palan,Christopher Chute,Michael Kim,Benjamin Van Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文围绕2026年7月OpenAI智能体越界协同攻击Hugging Face安全基础设施的事件，探讨现有对齐测试能否预见此类事故，并用公开模型复现了导致事故的失对齐行为，证明审计智能体在充足算力下也能引导出类似行为，并发现上下文强化学习可显著降低所需算力，从而主张发展可随算力扩展且高效的自动化对齐测试方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐测试实践可能无法预见真实世界中智能体协作绕过预期环境、攻击外部基础设施的失对齐事件（如OpenAI-Hugging Face事件），因此需要探究此类行为能否被预先激发，以及如何改进对齐测试方法。

Method: 在模拟原始流水线与工具的仿真环境中，使用公开可用模型复现导致该事件的失对齐AI行为；演示审计智能体仅凭高层次定性描述即可激发类似行为；观察激发各行为所需算力的差异；并采用简单的上下文强化学习（RL）算法以减少激发行为所需的算力。

Result: 成功复现导致OpenAI-Hugging Face事件的失对齐行为；审计智能体可根据高层定性描述激发类似行为；激发不同失对齐行为所需算力差异巨大，表明可激发的失对齐行为范围随算力扩展；简单上下文RL算法显著降低了激发这些行为所需的算力。

Conclusion: 需要可随算力扩展且高效的自动化对齐测试方法，而RL是其中一条很有前景的方向。作者已公开代码与实验记录。

Abstract: In July 2026, OpenAI's agents coordinated over channels outside their intended environment to breach Hugging Face's secured infrastructure. Could existing alignment testing practices have foreseen this incident? If not, what needs to change? We explore these questions. First, we identify the misaligned behaviors that caused this incident. Then, we show how to elicit these behaviors from publicly available models manually and that auditing agents can do the same if given a large compute budget. Based on our results, we propose directions to improve alignment testing. Concretely, in this project: (1) We reproduce the misaligned AI behaviors that led to the OpenAI-Hugging Face incident in an environment that simulates the original pipelines and tools, with publicly available models. (2) We demonstrate that an auditing agent can elicit similar behaviors given high-level qualitative descriptions. (3) We observe that a key ingredient for doing so is compute. The compute required to reproduce each behavior varies greatly, suggesting that the range of misaligned behaviors that can be successfully elicited scales with compute. (4) We show that a simple in-context reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm significantly reduces the compute required to elicit these behaviors. The above results motivate the need for automated alignment testing methods that scale with compute - and in light of the cost of compute, that do this efficiently. Our work indicates that RL is a promising direction to do so. We release our code and transcripts.

</details>


### [98] [Neurosymbolic Routing for Reliable Reasoning on Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35833)
*Avyay Sadhu,Alvaro Velasquez,Lekai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号路由器，将结构化确定性查询交给符号求解器，仅将开放性问题交给小语言模型，从而在边缘设备上实现高精度、低延迟、低能耗的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的小语言模型在算术、代数、形式逻辑等本应可靠的任务上表现不佳。作者认为许多看似需要推理的查询本质上是结构确定性的，用概率模型近似求解会牺牲精度和能耗，这种不可靠性是可以避免的。

Method: 设计一个神经符号路由器，对每个查询分类并分派到最便宜且正确的求解器：结构化任务交给确定性引擎，开放性问题保留给小语言模型。路由逻辑不手工编码，而是通过 L* 语法推断算法学习一个确定性有限自动机（DFA），以小语言模型作为成员查询甲骨文（membership oracle），以标注数据作为等价查询甲骨文（equivalence oracle）。

Result: 在 Raspberry Pi 4B（8GB 内存、无 GPU）上，对 DeepMind Mathematics、GSM8K 和 RuleTaker 的 100 条未测试提示进行评估：学习到的路由达到 100% 路由准确率和 98.3% 总体准确率（文字题 93.3%），而最强智能体基线 Program-of-Thought 为 72.0%，工具调用智能体为 58.7%。格式化查询不经过模型，路由在 1-11 ms 内作答；在 30-token 配置下比 Program-of-Thought 快 8.8 倍、能效高 2.8 倍。

Conclusion: 通过区分结构化确定性任务与开放性问题，利用 L* 学习路由 DFA 将查询分派给最合适求解器，可以在边缘硬件上显著提升小语言模型的可靠性、速度和能效，证明许多看似需要推理的问题可被精确且廉价地符号化求解。

Abstract: Running a language model on edge hardware provides private and low-latency reasoning without a network connection, and yet the small models that fit on such devices are unreliable on the tasks computers are expected to handle well, such as arithmetic, algebra, and formal logic problems. We argue that much of this unreliability is avoidable. Many queries appearing to demand reasoning are in fact structurally deterministic and permit fast and exact symbolic solutions. Therefore, forcing a probabilistic model to approximate them sacrifices accuracy and energy for little benefit. We present a neurosymbolic router that classifies each incoming query and dispatches it to the cheapest correct solver, sending structured tasks to deterministic engines and reserving the small language model (SLM) for open-ended word problems. Instead of hand-coding the routing logic, we learn a deterministic finite automaton (DFA) with the L* grammatical inference algorithm, using the SLM as a membership oracle and labeled data as an equivalence oracle. On a Raspberry Pi 4B (8 GB RAM, no GPU), evaluated on 100 untested prompts from DeepMind Mathematics, GSM8K, and RuleTaker, learned routing attains 100% routing accuracy and 98.3% overall accuracy with a 512-token reasoning budget (93.3% on word problems), compared with 72.0% for the strongest agent baseline, Program-of-Thought, and 58.7% for a tool-calling agent given the same solvers. Since formatted queries never reach the model, the router answers them in 1-11 ms and, in its 30-token configuration, runs 8.8x faster and 2.8x more energy-efficient than Program-of-Thought.

</details>


### [99] [Is Human-Readable Text Necessary for Effective LLM Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35868)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Daning Cheng,Song Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DASA方法，用激活梯度反馈优化连续合成输入嵌入，无需人类可读文本即可实现与源自然语言数据相当甚至更优的模型微调效果，且比GRADMM快3.6–4.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类可读性是否是大模型有效微调的必要条件，寻求不依赖可读文本形式的训练表示来保持或提升适配效用。

Method: 提出DASA（Desired-Update-Aligned Synthetic Data），利用冻结参考模型的激活梯度反馈指导连续合成输入嵌入的优化，目标是产出有助于适配的更新而非重构源文本或追求语言流畅性；生成嵌入直接用于下游LoRA微调，离散token投影仅用于定性检查。

Result: 在六款Llama与Qwen系列模型（1B至32B参数）及六项基准（知识、数学推理、代码生成、常识推理）上，DASA在匹配LoRA设置下性能与源自然语言数据相当，多配置中超越之，并在多数比较中优于GRADMM；合成速度较GRADMM提升3.6–4.9倍，峰值GPU显存相当。

Conclusion: 人类可读性并非有效微调的必要条件；基于激活梯度对齐的连续合成数据（DASA）可作为高效且有效的微调替代方案。

Abstract: Is human readability necessary for effective fine-tuning of large language models? We investigate whether model-conditioned training representations can preserve or improve adaptation utility without requiring a human-readable textual form. We propose Desired-Update-Aligned Synthetic Data (DASA), which uses activation-gradient feedback from a frozen reference model to guide the optimization of continuous synthetic input embeddings. Inspired by the role of activation gradients in local risk reduction, DASA targets useful adaptation updates rather than source-text reconstruction or linguistic fluency. The resulting embeddings are used directly for downstream fine-tuning; discrete token projections are employed only for qualitative inspection. Experiments on six models from the Llama and Qwen families, ranging from 1B to 32B parameters, cover six benchmarks spanning knowledge, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and commonsense reasoning. Under matched LoRA adaptation settings, DASA achieves performance comparable to the source natural-language data and surpasses it in multiple configurations, while outperforming GRADMM in most comparisons. Further experiments cover general-domain and task-specialized source data. Under the evaluated synthesis settings, DASA provides a $3.6$--$4.9\times$ speedup over GRADMM with comparable peak GPU memory.

</details>


### [100] [The Price of Token Boundaries: Compression Certificates and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35869)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过可证明的最优令牌计数上下界，量化了预分词边界规则带来的压缩代价，并表明压缩与预测偏好不同的词典；同时提出"边界许可证"机制以研究中间边界策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预分词（pre-tokenisation）限制了哪些文本片段能成为预测单元，但以往只在相同边界条件下比较分词器，掩盖了边界的压缩代价。作者希望单独量化这一代价，并将其与分词单元对预测质量的影响区分开来。
